% COMPILE WITH LUALATEX:
%     latexmk -lualatex mrl-trace-release.tex
%
% LuaLaTeX is required for two reasons and no others: Unicode in the printed
% listings, and the pure-Lua base64 decoder below, which reconstructs the three
% first-party .npz archives from the blocks at the end of this file and attaches
% them to the PDF. No shell escape, no network access, no external decoder and
% no adjacent ZIP are needed. The file compiles on its own.
% =========================================================================

% NO commandchars. The listings are true verbatim: a backslash or a brace in a
% Python source must reach the page as itself, not be executed as TeX. The
% listing style is set once here rather than on each Verbatim environment; line
% numbers restart at 1 in every one, which gives each file its own numbering.
\fvset{numbers=left,breaklines=true,breakanywhere=true,fontsize=\scriptsize,%
       numbersep=4pt,numberblanklines=false}

% ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
% Pure-Lua base64 decoder. Reads this very source, finds each marked block,
% strips the comment prefixes, decodes, and writes the archive to the build
% directory. No shell escape, no network, no external decoder, no adjacent ZIP.
%
% The Lua below deliberately uses no backslash, no percent sign and no hash.
% TeX tokenises the argument of \directlua before Lua ever sees it, so those
% three are the escape, the comment and the macro-parameter characters, and any
% of them would be consumed or would raise a TeX error. Hence string.char(10)
% for a newline, string.char(37) for a per cent sign, string.len instead of the
% length operator, math.fmod instead of the modulo operator, and plain
% (non-pattern) string.find throughout. The marker literals are assembled from
% pieces so that the scanner cannot match its own source.
% ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
\directlua{
local NL = string.char(10)
local PCT = string.char(37)
local B64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local rev = {}
for i = 1, 64 do rev[string.sub(B64, i, i)] = i - 1 end

local function decode(payload)
  local out, n, bits, nbits = {}, 0, 0, 0
  for i = 1, string.len(payload) do
    local c = string.sub(payload, i, i)
    if c == "=" then break end
    bits = bits * 64 + rev[c]
    nbits = nbits + 6
    if nbits >= 8 then
      nbits = nbits - 8
      local shift = 2 ^ nbits
      local byte = math.floor(bits / shift)
      bits = bits - byte * shift
      n = n + 1
      out[n] = string.char(byte)
    end
  end
  return table.concat(out)
end

local function source_text()
  local names = { tex.jobname .. ".tex" }
  local found = kpse.find_file(tex.jobname .. ".tex", "tex")
  if found then names[2] = found end
  for _, name in ipairs(names) do
    local fh = io.open(name, "rb")
    if fh then
      local text = fh:read("*a")
      fh:close()
      if string.find(text, "NPZ" .. "_BEGIN", 1, true) then return text, name end
    end
  end
  error("mrl supplement: cannot read the compiling source file. Expected "
        .. tex.jobname .. ".tex in the working directory, carrying the NPZ blocks.")
end

local text, where = source_text()
local OPEN = "<NPZ" .. "_BEGIN name=" .. string.char(34)
local SHUT = string.char(34) .. ">"
local STOP = PCT .. "<NPZ" .. "_END>"
local pos, count, report = 1, 0, {}

while true do
  local a, b = string.find(text, OPEN, pos, true)
  if not a then break end
  local c, d = string.find(text, SHUT, b + 1, true)
  if not c then
    error("mrl supplement: a block header is malformed -- no closing quote after "
          .. "character " .. b .. ".")
  end
  local name = string.sub(text, b + 1, c - 1)
  local e, f = string.find(text, STOP, d + 1, true)
  if not e then
    error("mrl supplement: the block for " .. name .. " has no end marker.")
  end
  local block = string.sub(text, d + 1, e - 1)

  local buf, k = {}, 0
  for i = 1, string.len(block) do
    local ch = string.sub(block, i, i)
    if rev[ch] ~= nil or ch == "=" then
      k = k + 1
      buf[k] = ch
    elseif ch ~= PCT and ch ~= NL and ch ~= " " and ch ~= string.char(13) then
      error("mrl supplement: the block for " .. name .. " contains a character that is "
            .. "neither base64 nor a comment prefix, at offset " .. i .. ".")
    end
  end
  local payload = table.concat(buf)
  if string.len(payload) == 0 then
    error("mrl supplement: the block for " .. name .. " is empty.")
  end
  if math.fmod(string.len(payload), 4) ~= 0 then
    error("mrl supplement: the block for " .. name .. " is truncated -- its payload is "
          .. string.len(payload) .. " characters, which is not a multiple of 4.")
  end
  local bytes = decode(payload)
  if string.sub(name, -4) == ".npz" and string.sub(bytes, 1, 2) ~= "PK" then
    error("mrl supplement: " .. name .. " did not decode to a zip container (an .npz "
          .. "begins PK), so the block is corrupt.")
  end
  local fh = io.open(name, "wb")
  if not fh then
    error("mrl supplement: cannot write " .. name .. " into the build directory.")
  end
  fh:write(bytes)
  fh:close()
  count = count + 1
  report[count] = name .. " (" .. string.len(bytes) .. " bytes)"
  pos = f + 1
end

if count == 0 then
  error("mrl supplement: no NPZ blocks were found in " .. where
        .. ". The evidence blocks are required; the document cannot attach them.")
end
texio.write_nl("mrl supplement: reconstructed " .. count .. " archives: "
               .. table.concat(report, ", "))
}
\begin{document}
\maketitle
\section{Package scope and reconstruction}
This document is the complete source of the software supporting Chapter~7, together with the
first-party numerical evidence its reported results are computed from. It is self-contained:
every listing below is printed in full, and the three deposited archives are embedded as
base64 at the end of the file.

The companion \texttt{mrl-trace-release.zip} beside this document is the same software as an
executable tree, and is the convenient way to run it. It is not needed to compile or read
this supplement.

\subsection{What is printed, and what is not}
Printed verbatim: every Python file under \texttt{src/}, \texttt{tools/} and \texttt{tests/};
\texttt{README.md}, \texttt{THIRD\_PARTY\_DATA.md} and \texttt{LICENSE};
\texttt{pyproject.toml} and \texttt{uv.lock}; the four JSON sidecars; and every notebook
markdown and code cell in execution order.

Not printed: the rendered figures, raster or vector, and every notebook output payload. Each
figure is drawn by the plotting code in Section~\ref{sec:plotting} from the arrays embedded
here, so the pictures carry no information the source and the evidence do not.

\subsection{Extracting the embedded evidence}
This is extraction, not regeneration. The Lua below recovers the deposited archives exactly as
they were packed, byte for byte; it refits nothing, resimulates nothing, and reads no raw
measurement. Recomputing the reported quantities \emph{from} those arrays is what
\texttt{tools/quantities.py} does, and rerunning the fits and the learners from scratch is what
the two training profiles of the adjacent package do.

Compiling this file with LuaLaTeX writes the three archives into the build directory and
attaches them to the resulting PDF:
\begin{quote}
\texttt{latexmk -lualatex mrl-trace-release.tex}
\end{quote}
The decoder is pure Lua, embedded in the preamble. It locates this source, finds each marked
block, strips the comment prefixes, decodes the payload, and stops with a stated reason if a
block is missing, empty, truncated or not a zip container. Reconstruction therefore needs no
shell escape, no network access, no external tool and no adjacent ZIP. Saving an attachment
and loading it needs nothing but NumPy:
\begin{quote}
\texttt{python -c "import numpy; print(numpy.load('mrl-device.npz',
allow\_pickle=False).files)"}
\end{quote}
The attachment links sit in the three evidence sections that follow, beside the tables
describing what each archive holds.
\clearpage
\tableofcontents
\clearpage
\section{Environment and third-party data}
The five external mesoporous-silica traces are not redistributed here in any form,
and neither the workbook nor the prepared cache is embedded. One figure needs them, and a
reader who obtains the workbook separately prepares it with the command in
\texttt{THIRD\_PARTY\_DATA.md}; \texttt{data/external.json} carries the worksheet name and the
column mapping the preparation reads. Without the workbook the reproduction command skips that
one figure with a stated instruction and everything else runs, which is why the adjacent ZIP
still carries the rendered external figure while this document embeds neither it nor the
measurements behind it.
\subsection{README.md}
\index{README.md}
\begin{Verbatim}
# Memristive Relaxation and Learning

This package contains measured silicon-oxide relaxations, fitted decay
parameters, temporal-credit simulations, multilayer learning results, and the
code used to analyse and plot them.

## Reproduce

Install the environment and regenerate the deposited results and figures:

```bash
uv sync
uv run python tools/reproduce.py
```

The default `archived` profile reads the deposited numerical evidence, prints
the principal quantities, and regenerates the figures. It performs no training.

Two training profiles use the same scientific drivers with different budgets:

```bash
uv run python tools/reproduce.py reduced
uv run python tools/reproduce.py published
```

`reduced` is a quick end-to-end smoke test. `published` runs the complete
current protocols. Fresh runs are written under `outputs/`, which is not part
of the release archive.

The `published` profile does not reconstruct an earlier production pipeline.
For the multilayer experiment, a fresh run may therefore differ in magnitude
from the deposited result while retaining its tested comparison. The
`archived` profile reproduces the deposited quantities exactly.

## Contents

- `data/`: first-party measurements, fits, uncertainty regions, and simulation
  results in pickle-free NumPy archives.
- `figures/`: rendered figures.
- `notebooks/`: three executed walkthroughs.
- `src/mrl_trace/`: fitting, kernel, statistics, and learning code.
- `src/mrl_trace/plotting/`: shared figure producers.
- `tools/`: the reproduction command, external-data preparation, and quantity
  calculations.
- `tests/`: package and numerical checks.

The independent mesoporous-silica measurements are not redistributed. See
`THIRD_PARTY_DATA.md`.
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{THIRD\_PARTY\_DATA.md}
\index{THIRD\_PARTY\_DATA.md}
\begin{Verbatim}
# External Mesoporous-Silica Measurements

The five external decay traces are available from the University of
Southampton repository under DOI `10.5258/SOTON/D2975`. This package does not
redistribute the workbook.

After downloading the workbook, prepare the local cache with:

```bash
uv run python tools/prepare_data.py external /path/to/workbook.xlsx
uv run python tools/reproduce.py
```

The preparation command reads worksheet `Figure8` and the column pairs listed
in `data/external.json`. It writes a fitted local cache to
`~/.cache/mrl-trace/external_mesoporous.npz`. The cache is outside this package
and is never included in the ZIP.
\end{Verbatim}

\index{pyproject.toml}
\begin{Verbatim}
[build-system]
requires = ["setuptools>=77"]
build-backend = "setuptools.build_meta"

[project]
name = "mrl-trace"
version = "1.0.0"
description = "Measured relaxation kernels and reward-modulated learning"
readme = "README.md"
license = "MIT"
license-files = ["LICENSE"]
requires-python = ">=3.10,<3.13"
dependencies = [
    "matplotlib>=3.7",
    "numpy>=1.24",
    "scipy>=1.10",
]

[project.optional-dependencies]
test = [
    "ipykernel>=6.25",
    "nbclient>=0.8",
    "nbformat>=5.9",
    "pytest>=7.4",
]

[tool.setuptools.packages.find]
where = ["src"]

[tool.pytest.ini_options]
testpaths = ["tests"]
pythonpath = ["tools"]
\end{Verbatim}

\section{Device relaxation and kernel identification evidence}
\label{sec:device}
\texttt{device.npz} holds the ten measured relaxations, the screening result that
accepts nine of them, the accepted stretched-exponential fits, the sensitivity fits under a
changed floor convention and a shortened interval, and the profile-likelihood uncertainty
regions. The three floor conventions named in the arrays are the full-duration final tenth,
a floor fitted over the selected interval, and that interval's own final tenth; the
\texttt{fit\_convention} and \texttt{robust\_convention} columns record which produced each
cell.
\textattachfile{mrl-device.npz}{\texttt{mrl-device.npz}} \quad 60,708 bytes, reconstructed during compilation and attached to this PDF.

geometries are named in the \texttt{description} and \texttt{geometry} columns, and

learner without homeostasis, and the same learner without the eligibility trace.

\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/archives.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/archives.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Small, pickle-free readers for the deposited numerical evidence."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DATA = ROOT / "data"
NAMES = ("device", "credit", "multilayer")


def load(name: str):
    if name not in NAMES:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown archive {name!r}; expected one of {NAMES}")
    return np.load(DATA / f"{name}.npz", allow_pickle=False)


def metadata(name: str) -> dict:
    path = DATA / f"{name}.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise KeyError(f"no metadata for {name!r}")
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def external_cache() -> Path:
    return Path("~/.cache/mrl-trace/external_mesoporous.npz").expanduser()
\end{Verbatim}

"""Registered scientific figure names shared by the notebooks and CLI."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
FIGURE_DIR = ROOT / "figures"

FIGURES = {
    "measured_relaxation": "measured_relaxation",
    "eligibility_kernel_physics": "eligibility_kernel_physics",
    "kernel_reliability": "kernel_reliability",
    "kernel_reliability_lower": "kernel_reliability",
    "window_external": "window_external",
    "role_swap_task": "role_swap_task",
    "credit_threshold_witness": "credit_threshold_witness",
    "learned_credit": "learned_credit",
    "three_factor_physics": "three_factor_physics",
    "multilayer_agent": "multilayer_agent",
    "multilayer_homeostasis": "multilayer_homeostasis",
    "fig_eligibility_origin": "eligibility_origin",
    "fig_task": "role_swap_task",
    "fig_protocol": "protocol",
    "fig_measured_records": "measured_relaxation",
    "fig_learning": "learned_credit",
    "fig_reliability": "kernel_reliability",
    "fig_s1_window_external": "window_external",
}


def save(fig, stem: str, *, raster: bool = True) -> None:
    FIGURE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pad = getattr(fig, "_release_pad_inches", None)
    fig.savefig(FIGURE_DIR / f"{stem}.pdf", bbox_inches="tight", pad_inches=pad)
    if raster:
        fig.savefig(
            FIGURE_DIR / f"{stem}.png", dpi=220, bbox_inches="tight", pad_inches=pad)
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/identification.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/identification.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import least_squares
from .archives import load
WINDOWS_S = (20.0, 30.0, 60.0, 120.0, 160.0, 226.0)
CONVENTIONS = ('full_duration_floor', 'free_floor', 'window_tail_floor')

def mean_retention(tau_s: float, beta: float) -> float:
    return tau_s / beta * math.gamma(1.0 / beta)

def _fixed_floor_fit(time, current, floor_current, *, robust=False):
    scale = float(np.max(current))
    y = current / scale
    floor = float(floor_current) / scale

    def model(parameters):
        amplitude, tau, beta = np.exp(parameters)
        return amplitude * np.exp(-np.power(time / tau, beta)) + floor
    initial = np.log([max(float(y[0] - floor), 0.05), max(float(np.median(time[time > 0])), 0.1), 0.6])
    result = least_squares(lambda parameters: model(parameters) - y, initial, bounds=(np.log([1e-05, 0.01, 0.1]), np.log([2.0, 10000.0, 2.0])), loss='soft_l1' if robust else 'linear', f_scale=0.03, max_nfev=5000)
    amplitude, tau, beta = np.exp(result.x)
    fitted = model(result.x)
    spread = float(np.sum(np.square(y - y.mean())))
    r_squared = 1.0 - float(np.sum(np.square(y - fitted))) / spread
    return (amplitude * scale, tau, beta, floor * scale, r_squared)

def _free_floor_fit(time, current, *, robust=False):
    scale = float(np.max(current))
    y = current / scale
    tail = float(np.median(y[-max(5, y.size // 10):]))

    def model(parameters):
        amplitude, tau, beta, floor = np.exp(parameters)
        return amplitude * np.exp(-np.power(time / tau, beta)) + floor
    initial = np.log([max(float(y[0] - tail), 0.05), max(float(np.median(time[time > 0])), 0.1), 0.6, max(tail, 1e-08)])
    result = least_squares(lambda parameters: model(parameters) - y, initial, bounds=(np.log([1e-05, 0.01, 0.1, 1e-08]), np.log([2.0, 10000.0, 2.0, 2.0])), loss='soft_l1' if robust else 'linear', f_scale=0.03, max_nfev=5000)
    amplitude, tau, beta, floor = np.exp(result.x)
    fitted = model(result.x)
    spread = float(np.sum(np.square(y - y.mean())))
    r_squared = 1.0 - float(np.sum(np.square(y - fitted))) / spread
    return (amplitude * scale, tau, beta, floor * scale, r_squared)

def fit_relaxations(*, runs=None, windows=WINDOWS_S, robust=False, progress=None) -> dict:
    with load('device') as archive:
        accepted = [str(run) for run, keep in zip(archive['run'], archive['accepted']) if keep]
        selected = accepted if runs is None else [str(run) for run in runs]
        missing = sorted(set(selected) - set(accepted))
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f'runs are not accepted measurements: {missing}')
        trace_run = np.asarray(archive['trace_run'], dtype=str)
        trace_time = np.asarray(archive['trace_time_s'], dtype=float)
        trace_current = np.asarray(archive['trace_current_a'], dtype=float)
    rows = []
    for run in selected:
        mask = trace_run == run
        time, current = (trace_time[mask], trace_current[mask])
        order = np.argsort(time)
        time, current = (time[order], current[order])
        full_floor = float(np.median(current[-max(5, current.size // 10):]))
        if progress is not None:
            progress(run)
        for window in windows:
            keep = time <= float(window) + 1e-09
            sample_time, sample_current = (time[keep], current[keep])
            if robust and sample_time.size > 300:
                index = np.linspace(0, sample_time.size - 1, 300).round().astype(int)
                sample_time, sample_current = (sample_time[index], sample_current[index])
            window_floor = float(np.median(sample_current[-max(5, sample_current.size // 10):]))
            for convention in CONVENTIONS:
                if convention == 'free_floor':
                    fitted = _free_floor_fit(sample_time, sample_current, robust=robust)
                else:
                    floor = full_floor if convention == 'full_duration_floor' else window_floor
                    fitted = _fixed_floor_fit(sample_time, sample_current, floor, robust=robust)
                amplitude, tau, beta, floor, r_squared = fitted
                rows.append({'run': run, 'window_s': float(window), 'convention': convention, 'tau_s': tau, 'beta': beta, 'mean_retention_s': mean_retention(tau, beta), 'amplitude_a': amplitude, 'floor_a': floor, 'r_squared': r_squared, 'n_samples': len(sample_time), 'robust': robust})
    return {'rows': rows, 'protocol': {'runs': selected, 'windows_s': list(map(float, windows)), 'conventions': list(CONVENTIONS), 'robust': robust}}
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/kernels.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/kernels.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import math
from typing import Callable, Sequence
from scipy.integrate import quad
CALIBRATION_FRACTIONS = (0.1, 0.5)
EVALUATION_FRACTIONS = (0.25, 0.9)
EVENT_WIDTH_FRACTION = 0.02

def window_average(q: Callable[[float], float], t: float, duration: float) -> float:
    if duration <= 0:
        raise ValueError('event duration must be positive')
    value, _ = quad(q, t - duration, t, limit=200)
    return value / duration

def finite_event_response(q: Callable[[float], float], a: float, d: float) -> float:
    return window_average(q, a, d)

def geometry_for(interval: Sequence[float], fractions: Sequence[float]) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    low, high = (float(interval[0]), float(interval[1]))
    span = high - low
    if span <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f'interval must have positive span, got {interval!r}')
    return (low + fractions[0] * span, low + fractions[1] * span, EVENT_WIDTH_FRACTION * span)

def credit_threshold(q: Callable[[float], float], a: float, b: float, d: float) -> float:
    return finite_event_response(q, a, d) / finite_event_response(q, b, d)

def task_equivalent_lifetime(q: Callable[[float], float], a: float, b: float, d: float) -> float:
    if not b > a:
        raise ValueError('require b > a')
    return (b - a) / math.log(credit_threshold(q, a, b, d))

def surrogate_gain(q: Callable[[float], float], a0: float, d: float, tau_0: float) -> float:
    return finite_event_response(q, a0, d) / finite_event_response(lambda t: math.exp(-float(t) / tau_0), a0, d)

def surrogate(tau_0: float, gain: float) -> Callable[[float], float]:
    if tau_0 <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError(f'tau_0 must be positive, got {tau_0}')
    return lambda t: gain * math.exp(-float(t) / tau_0)

def matched_surrogate(q: Callable[[float], float], interval: Sequence[float]) -> dict:
    a0, b0, d = geometry_for(interval, CALIBRATION_FRACTIONS)
    a1, b1, _ = geometry_for(interval, EVALUATION_FRACTIONS)
    tau_0 = task_equivalent_lifetime(q, a0, b0, d)
    gain = surrogate_gain(q, a0, d, tau_0)
    q_s = surrogate(tau_0, gain)
    physical = {'Q_a0': finite_event_response(q, a0, d), 'Q_b0': finite_event_response(q, b0, d), 'Q_a1': finite_event_response(q, a1, d), 'Q_b1': finite_event_response(q, b1, d)}
    matched = {'Q_a0': finite_event_response(q_s, a0, d), 'Q_b0': finite_event_response(q_s, b0, d), 'Q_a1': finite_event_response(q_s, a1, d), 'Q_b1': finite_event_response(q_s, b1, d)}
    residual_a0 = abs(physical['Q_a0'] - matched['Q_a0']) / physical['Q_a0']
    residual_b0 = abs(physical['Q_b0'] - matched['Q_b0']) / physical['Q_b0']
    r_physical = physical['Q_a1'] / physical['Q_b1']
    r_surrogate = matched['Q_a1'] / matched['Q_b1']
    return {'calibration_geometry': {'a': a0, 'b': b0, 'd': d, 'fractions': list(CALIBRATION_FRACTIONS)}, 'evaluation_geometry': {'a': a1, 'b': b1, 'd': d, 'fractions': list(EVALUATION_FRACTIONS)}, 'task_equivalent_lifetime_s': tau_0, 'gain': gain, 'has_offset_parameter': False, 'why_no_offset': '(A, t_min) is degenerate: t_min = a0-d and t_min = 0 give different gains that both match, so the gauge is fixed by dropping the offset', 'physical_responses': physical, 'surrogate_responses': matched, 'calibration_residual_relative': {'a0': residual_a0, 'b0': residual_b0}, 'matched_at_calibration': bool(max(residual_a0, residual_b0) < 1e-12), 'credit_threshold_at_evaluation': {'physical': r_physical, 'surrogate': r_surrogate, 'separation': r_physical / r_surrogate}, 'separated_at_evaluation': bool(abs(r_physical / r_surrogate - 1.0) > 1e-09), 'attribution': 'both responses agree at the calibration geometry, so any divergence at the evaluation geometry is attributable to kernel shape and not to update amplitude'}

def task_signature(q: Callable[[float], float], interval: Sequence[float], geometries: Sequence[Sequence[float]] | None=None) -> dict:
    pairs = list(geometries) if geometries is not None else [CALIBRATION_FRACTIONS, EVALUATION_FRACTIONS]
    out = {}
    for fractions in pairs:
        a, b, d = geometry_for(interval, fractions)
        out[f'{fractions[0]:.2f}-{fractions[1]:.2f}'] = {'ages': [a, b], 'event_width': d, 'Q_recent': finite_event_response(q, a, d), 'Q_older': finite_event_response(q, b, d), 'credit_threshold': credit_threshold(q, a, b, d)}
    return out

def kww_with_mean_retention(beta: float, mean_retention_s: float) -> tuple[float, Callable[[float], float]]:
    if beta <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError('beta must be positive')
    tau = mean_retention_s * beta / math.gamma(1.0 / beta)
    return (tau, lambda t, tau=tau, beta=beta: math.exp(-(max(float(t), 1e-12) / tau) ** beta))

def mean_retention(tau: float, beta: float) -> float:
    return tau / beta * math.gamma(1.0 / beta)

def scalar_insufficiency_witness(mean_retention_s: float, *, ages: tuple[float, float]=(1.0, 10.0), event_width_s: float=0.3, betas: Sequence[float]=(0.54, 0.621547, 0.85, 1.0, 1.3)) -> dict:
    a, b = ages
    rows = []
    for beta in betas:
        tau, q = kww_with_mean_retention(beta, mean_retention_s)
        rows.append({'beta': float(beta), 'tau_s': tau, 'mean_retention_s': mean_retention(tau, beta), 'credit_threshold': credit_threshold(q, a, b, event_width_s)})
    thresholds = [row['credit_threshold'] for row in rows]
    low, high = (min(thresholds), max(thresholds))
    scalars = [row['mean_retention_s'] for row in rows]
    return {'scalar': 'mean retention <t> = (tau/beta) Gamma(1/beta)', 'shared_scalar_s': mean_retention_s, 'scalar_identical_to': max((abs(s - mean_retention_s) for s in scalars)), 'ages_s': [a, b], 'event_width_s': event_width_s, 'kernels': rows, 'disagreement_interval': [low, high], 'interval_is_non_empty': bool(high > low), 'interval_width': high - low, 'spread': high / low, 'reading': 'for any event-strength ratio strictly inside the interval, these kernels credit opposite events while sharing one scalar exactly, so the scalar does not determine the temporal-credit operator', 'constructive': 'the kernels are constructed to share the scalar rather than found to share it, so this exhibits a counterexample and does not estimate a frequency'}
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/membrane.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/membrane.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
__all__ = ['TAU_M', 'V_TH', 'W_INIT', 'W_MAX', 'LTD_BIAS', 'lif_step_batched', 'relax_gate', 'relax_gate_ticks']
TAU_M = 0.02
V_TH = 1.0
W_INIT, W_MAX = (0.5, 1.5)
LTD_BIAS = 0.6

def lif_step_batched(v, charge, dt, rng, *, tau_m=TAU_M, v_th=V_TH, noise=0.15, v_reset=0.0, return_pre=False):
    v_pre = v + charge - dt * v / tau_m + noise * rng.standard_normal(v.shape)
    spike = v_pre >= v_th
    v = np.where(spike, v_reset, v_pre)
    if return_pre:
        return (v, spike, v_pre)
    return (v, spike)

def relax_gate(bank, n_steps, dt, stride=10):
    if n_steps <= 0:
        shape = bank.e.shape if hasattr(bank, 'e') else bank.vn.shape[:-1]
        return bank.step(np.zeros(shape))
    base = bank.dt
    coarse = max(1, n_steps // stride)
    bank.dt = base * (n_steps / coarse)
    shape = bank.e.shape if hasattr(bank, 'e') else bank.vn.shape[:-1]
    zero = np.zeros(shape)
    out = None
    try:
        for _ in range(coarse):
            out = bank.step(zero)
    finally:
        bank.dt = base
    return out

def relax_gate_ticks(bank, n_coarse, stride, remainder):
    shape = bank.e.shape if hasattr(bank, 'e') else bank.vn.shape[:-1]
    zero = np.zeros(shape)
    if n_coarse > 0:
        base = bank.dt
        bank.dt = base * stride
        try:
            for _ in range(n_coarse):
                bank.step(zero)
        finally:
            bank.dt = base
    for _ in range(remainder):
        bank.step(zero)
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/multilayer.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/multilayer.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import numpy as np
from .membrane import LTD_BIAS, TAU_M, V_TH, W_MAX, lif_step_batched, relax_gate_ticks
from .role_swap import KWWTrace
__all__ = ['MODES', 'HYPERPARAMETERS', 'HOMEOSTATIC_STRENGTH', 'DISTRACTOR', 'CONDITIONS', 'DECLARED', 'FINAL_FRACTION', 'GATE_KERNEL', 'decay_only_gate', 'xor_inputs', 'gate_readout', 'train', 'reward_rate', 'run_multilayer']
MODES = ('dfa', 'no_trace')
HYPERPARAMETERS = dict(H=32, D=3.0, eta=0.32, eta_hidden=3.0, fb_scale=2.0, bias_o=0.3, sigma0=0.15, sigma1=0.05)
HOMEOSTATIC_STRENGTH = 0.1
DISTRACTOR = {'t_distract': 2.0, 'distract_dur': 0.3}
CONDITIONS = (('dfa_homeo_dist', 'dfa', HOMEOSTATIC_STRENGTH, True), ('dfa_dist', 'dfa', 0.0, True), ('no_trace_homeo_dist', 'no_trace', HOMEOSTATIC_STRENGTH, True))
DECLARED = {'lanes': 20, 'trials': 2000}
FINAL_FRACTION = 0.1
GATE_KERNEL = {'tau_s': 16.01085496325088, 'beta': 0.6215469546368932}

def decay_only_gate(batch, states, actions, dt):
    return KWWTrace(batch, states, actions, dt=dt, **GATE_KERNEL)

def xor_inputs(rng, batch):
    b0 = rng.integers(2, size=batch)
    b1 = rng.integers(2, size=batch)
    lines = np.zeros((batch, 4))
    index = np.arange(batch)
    lines[index, 0 + b0] = 1.0
    lines[index, 2 + b1] = 1.0
    return (lines, (b0 ^ b1).astype(int))

def gate_readout(bank):
    if hasattr(bank, 'readout'):
        return bank.readout()
    raise TypeError(f'{type(bank).__name__} defines no readout(); an injected gate must expose one')

def reward_rate(rewards, window=200):
    rewards = np.asarray(rewards)
    if rewards.shape[-1] < window:
        return rewards.mean(axis=-1)
    return rewards[..., -window:].mean(axis=-1)

def train(*, gate_factory, mode='dfa', B=20, H=8, D=5.0, trials=2000, dt=0.005, cue_dur=1.0, eta=0.2, eta_hidden=None, in_rate=200.0, ltd=LTD_BIAS, tau_m=TAU_M, v_th=V_TH, sigma0=0.15, sigma1=0.05, fb_scale=1.0, w_scale1=0.6, w_scale2=0.35, w_max=W_MAX, bias_o=0.35, homeo=0.0, homeo_target=0.35, homeo_tau=200.0, t_distract=None, distract_dur=0.3, seed0=0):
    if mode not in MODES:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown mode {mode!r}; have {MODES}')
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed0)
    F, A = (4, 2)
    eta_h = eta if eta_hidden is None else eta_hidden
    no_trace = mode == 'no_trace'

    def _init(shape, scale):
        return np.clip(scale * rng.standard_normal(shape), -w_max, w_max)
    W1 = _init((B, F, H), w_scale1)
    W2 = _init((B, H, A), w_scale2)
    g1 = gate_factory(B, F, H, dt)
    g_out = gate_factory(B, H, A, dt)
    B_fix = fb_scale * rng.standard_normal((A, H))
    baseline = np.full(B, 1.0 / A)
    bidx = np.arange(B)
    cue = (0.3, 0.3 + cue_dur)
    reward_lag = int(round(D / dt))
    nsteps = int(round(cue[1] / dt)) + 2
    rewards = np.zeros((B, trials))
    act_hidden = np.full((B, H), homeo_target) if homeo > 0 else None
    for tr in range(trials):
        sigma = sigma0 + (sigma1 - sigma0) * tr / trials
        g1.reset()
        g_out.reset()
        lines, correct = xor_inputs(rng, B)
        vh = np.zeros((B, H))
        vo = np.zeros((B, A))
        spk_o = np.zeros((B, A))
        spk_h = np.zeros((B, H))
        for n in range(nsteps):
            t = n * dt
            on = cue[0] <= t < cue[1]
            pre_in = (rng.random((B, F)) < in_rate * dt * lines).astype(float) if on else np.zeros((B, F))
            ch_h = np.einsum('bfh,bf->bh', W1, pre_in)
            vh, sp_h = lif_step_batched(vh, ch_h, dt, rng, tau_m=tau_m, v_th=v_th, noise=sigma)
            spk_h += sp_h
            ch_o = np.einsum('bha,bh->ba', W2, sp_h.astype(float))
            if on:
                ch_o = ch_o + bias_o
            vo, sp_o = lif_step_batched(vo, ch_o, dt, rng, tau_m=tau_m, v_th=v_th, noise=sigma)
            spk_o += sp_o
            if no_trace:
                g1.step(np.zeros((B, F, H)))
                g_out.step(np.zeros((B, H, A)))
            else:
                g1.step(pre_in[:, :, None] * np.where(sp_h, 1.0, -ltd)[:, None, :])
                g_out.step(sp_h.astype(float)[:, :, None] * np.where(sp_o, 1.0, -ltd)[:, None, :])
        if not no_trace:
            stride = 10 if reward_lag > 200 else 1
            if t_distract is None:
                n_coarse, rem = divmod(reward_lag, stride)
                relax_gate_ticks(g_out, n_coarse, stride, rem)
                relax_gate_ticks(g1, n_coarse, stride, rem)
            else:
                i_d0 = min(int(round(t_distract / dt)), reward_lag)
                i_d1 = min(i_d0 + int(round(distract_dur / dt)), reward_lag)
                n0, r0 = divmod(i_d0, stride)
                n1, r1 = divmod(reward_lag - i_d1, stride)
                relax_gate_ticks(g_out, n0, stride, r0)
                relax_gate_ticks(g1, n0, stride, r0)
                for _ in range(i_d1 - i_d0):
                    dfire = (rng.random((B, F)) < in_rate * dt).astype(float)
                    hfire = (rng.random((B, H)) < in_rate * dt).astype(float)
                    g1.step(dfire[:, :, None] * np.where(hfire, 1.0, -ltd)[:, None, :])
                    ofire = (rng.random((B, A)) < in_rate * dt).astype(float)
                    g_out.step(hfire[:, :, None] * np.where(ofire, 1.0, -ltd)[:, None, :])
                relax_gate_ticks(g_out, n1, stride, r1)
                relax_gate_ticks(g1, n1, stride, r1)
            eo_rew = gate_readout(g_out)
            e1_rew = gate_readout(g1)
        else:
            eo_rew = np.zeros_like(gate_readout(g_out))
            e1_rew = np.zeros((B, F, H))
        tie = spk_o.max(1) == spk_o.min(1)
        chosen = np.argmax(spk_o, 1)
        chosen[tie] = rng.integers(A, size=int(tie.sum()))
        reward = (chosen == correct).astype(float)
        adv = reward - baseline
        Lo = adv[:, None, None]
        logits = spk_o - spk_o.mean(1, keepdims=True)
        pol = np.exp(logits - logits.max(1, keepdims=True))
        pol /= pol.sum(1, keepdims=True)
        onehot = np.zeros((B, A))
        onehot[bidx, chosen] = 1.0
        Lh = np.einsum('ah,ba->bh', B_fix, adv[:, None] * (onehot - pol))[:, None, :]
        W2 = np.clip(W2 + eta * Lo * eo_rew, -w_max, w_max)
        dW1 = eta_h * Lh * e1_rew
        if homeo > 0 and act_hidden is not None:
            rate = spk_h / nsteps
            act_hidden += (rate - act_hidden) / homeo_tau
            scale = 1.0 + homeo * (homeo_target - act_hidden)
            dW1 = dW1 + (scale[:, None, :] - 1.0) * W1
        W1 = np.clip(W1 + dW1, -w_max, w_max)
        baseline += 0.02 * (reward - baseline)
        rewards[:, tr] = reward
    return rewards

def run_multilayer(*, lanes: int, trials: int, conditions=CONDITIONS, progress=None) -> dict:
    if trials < 2:
        raise ValueError('trials must be at least two')
    lanes, trials = (int(lanes), int(trials))
    window = max(1, int(round(trials * FINAL_FRACTION)))
    rows, curves, finals = ([], {}, {})
    for label, mode, homeo, distract in conditions:
        if progress is not None:
            progress(label)
        kw = dict(HYPERPARAMETERS)
        kw['homeo'] = homeo
        if distract:
            kw.update(DISTRACTOR)
        rewards = train(gate_factory=decay_only_gate, mode=mode, B=lanes, trials=trials, seed0=0, **kw)
        final = reward_rate(rewards, window=window)
        finals[label] = final
        curves[label] = rewards.mean(0)
        for lane in range(lanes):
            rows.append({'condition': label, 'run': lane, 'final_reward': float(final[lane])})
    return {'rows': rows, 'curves': curves, 'finals': finals, 'protocol': {'lanes': lanes, 'trials': trials, 'final_window': window, 'hyperparameters': dict(HYPERPARAMETERS), 'gate': {'kind': 'decay_only_kww_mode_bank', **GATE_KERNEL}, 'distractor': dict(DISTRACTOR)}}
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/role\_swap.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/role\_swap.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import nnls
from .membrane import W_INIT, W_MAX, lif_step_batched, relax_gate as _relax
__all__ = ['TIMING', 'GEOMETRIES', 'AGE_PAIRS', 'DEFAULT_AGE_PAIR', 'MIXTURE', 'DEVICE_CONDITIONS', 'MEMBRANE_RESIDUAL_TOLERANCE', 'STREAM_SEED', 'GEOMETRY_SEED', 'ExactKernel', 'KWWTrace', 'StreamBundle', 'TrialBlockResult', 'named_streams', 'balanced_schedule', 'run_trial_block', 'timing_for', 'evaluation_timing_for', 'DECLARED', 'ETA', 'WINDOW_ARMS', 'ARM_NORMALIZERS', 'DECLARED_WEIGHTS', 'ENDPOINTS', 'GATE_TIERS', 'sign_flip_floor', 'arms_from_fits', 'exact_bank', 'run_role_swap']
DEVICE_CONDITIONS = {'in_rate': 200.0, 'tau_m': 0.02, 'v_th': 1.0, 'sigma': 0.15, 'ltd': 0.6}
MEMBRANE_RESIDUAL_TOLERANCE = 1e-06
TIMING = {'old_event_start_s': 0.0, 'old_event_end_s': 0.3, 'recent_event_start_s': 9.0, 'recent_event_end_s': 9.3, 'reward_s': 10.0, 'event_dur_s': 0.3, 'dt_s': 0.005, 'old_age_at_reward_s': 10.0, 'recent_age_at_reward_s': 1.0}
GEOMETRIES = ('old_informative', 'recent_informative')
AGE_PAIRS = {'g1': {'recent_age_s': 1.0, 'older_age_s': 10.0, 'tuned_exponential_tau_needed_s': 15.847, 'window_arm_r_star_gap': 0.485}, 'g2': {'recent_age_s': 3.0, 'older_age_s': 20.0, 'tuned_exponential_tau_needed_s': 21.37, 'window_arm_r_star_gap': 2.097}}
DEFAULT_AGE_PAIR = 'g1'
EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS = {'h1': {'recent_age_s': 1.5, 'older_age_s': 11.0, 'placement': 'interpolates between the development pairs on both ages'}, 'h2': {'recent_age_s': 3.5, 'older_age_s': 19.0, 'placement': "mildly extrapolates on the recent age (3.5 against g2's 3.0)"}}
MIXTURE = {'modes': 24, 'log_span': (-3.0, 2.0), 'grid_horizon_s': 60.0, 'grid_step_s': 0.02}

def timing_for(age_pair: str, *, event_dur_s: float=0.3) -> dict:
    if age_pair not in AGE_PAIRS:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown age pair {age_pair!r}')
    recent, older = (AGE_PAIRS[age_pair]['recent_age_s'], AGE_PAIRS[age_pair]['older_age_s'])
    if not 0.0 < recent < older:
        raise ValueError(f'ages must satisfy 0 < recent < older, got {recent} and {older}')
    reward = older
    recent_onset = reward - recent
    if recent_onset < event_dur_s:
        raise ValueError(f'the recent event would overlap the older one at {age_pair}')
    return {'old_event_start_s': 0.0, 'old_event_end_s': event_dur_s, 'recent_event_start_s': recent_onset, 'recent_event_end_s': recent_onset + event_dur_s, 'reward_s': reward, 'event_dur_s': event_dur_s, 'dt_s': TIMING['dt_s'], 'old_age_at_reward_s': older, 'recent_age_at_reward_s': recent, 'age_pair': age_pair}

def evaluation_timing_for(age_pair: str, *, event_dur_s: float=0.3) -> dict:
    if age_pair not in EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown evaluation age pair {age_pair!r}; expected one of {sorted(EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS)}')
    block = EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS[age_pair]
    recent, older = (block['recent_age_s'], block['older_age_s'])
    if not 0.0 < recent < older:
        raise ValueError(f'ages must satisfy 0 < recent < older, got {recent} and {older}')
    reward = older
    recent_onset = reward - recent
    if recent_onset < event_dur_s:
        raise ValueError(f'the recent event would overlap the older one at {age_pair}')
    return {'old_event_start_s': 0.0, 'old_event_end_s': event_dur_s, 'recent_event_start_s': recent_onset, 'recent_event_end_s': recent_onset + event_dur_s, 'reward_s': reward, 'event_dur_s': event_dur_s, 'dt_s': TIMING['dt_s'], 'old_age_at_reward_s': older, 'recent_age_at_reward_s': recent, 'age_pair': age_pair}
STREAM_SEED = {'published': 1049638075, 'reduced': 2815284313}
GEOMETRY_SEED = {'old_informative': 2554526018, 'recent_informative': 2070371314}

@dataclass
class StreamBundle:
    labels: np.random.Generator
    events: np.random.Generator
    explore: np.random.Generator
    init: np.random.Generator
    entropy: int = 0
    PURPOSES = ('labels', 'events', 'explore', 'init')

def named_streams(geometry: str, stream: str) -> StreamBundle:
    if geometry not in GEOMETRIES:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown geometry {geometry!r}')
    if stream not in STREAM_SEED:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown stream {stream!r}; have {sorted(STREAM_SEED)}')
    entropy = STREAM_SEED[stream]
    children = np.random.SeedSequence([entropy, GEOMETRY_SEED[geometry]]).spawn(4)
    return StreamBundle(*(np.random.default_rng(child) for child in children), entropy=entropy)

def balanced_schedule(trials: int, rng: np.random.Generator):
    if trials < 2 or trials % 2:
        raise ValueError(f'balanced schedule needs an even trial count >= 2, got {trials}')
    classes = np.arange(trials) % 2
    actions = classes.copy()
    actions[trials // 2:] = 1 - actions[trials // 2:]
    order = rng.permutation(trials)
    return (classes[order][None, :], actions[order][None, :])

class KWWTrace:

    def __init__(self, batch: int, states: int, actions: int, *, tau_s: float, beta: float, dt: float):
        exponents = np.linspace(*MIXTURE['log_span'], MIXTURE['modes'])
        exponents[int(np.argmin(np.abs(exponents)))] = 0.0
        self.taus = float(tau_s) * 10.0 ** exponents
        lags = np.arange(0.0, MIXTURE['grid_horizon_s'] + 1e-09, MIXTURE['grid_step_s'])
        target = np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(lags, 1e-12) / float(tau_s), float(beta)))
        basis = np.exp(-lags[:, None] / self.taus[None, :])
        weights, _ = nnls(basis, target)
        total = weights.sum()
        self.weights = weights / total if total > 0 else weights
        self.fit_error = float(np.abs(target - basis @ self.weights).max())
        self.tau_s, self.beta, self.dt = (float(tau_s), float(beta), float(dt))
        self.B, self.S, self.A = (batch, states, actions)
        self.vn = np.zeros((batch, states, actions, self.taus.size))

    def reset(self) -> None:
        self.vn[:] = 0.0

    def step(self, drive):
        decay = np.exp(-self.dt / self.taus)
        self.vn = self.vn * decay + self.dt * np.asarray(drive)[..., None]
        return self.readout()

    def readout(self):
        return np.clip(self.vn @ self.weights, -1.0, 1.0)

class ExactKernel:

    def __init__(self, batch: int, states: int, actions: int, *, tau_s: float, beta: float, dt: float, horizon_s: float=60.0):
        if tau_s <= 0.0 or beta < 0.0:
            raise ValueError(f'need tau > 0 and beta >= 0, got tau={tau_s} beta={beta}')
        self.tau_s, self.beta, self.dt = (float(tau_s), float(beta), float(dt))
        self.horizon_s = float(horizon_s)
        self.B, self.S, self.A = (batch, states, actions)
        self.vn = np.zeros((batch, states, actions, 1))
        self.fit_error = 0.0
        self._deposits: list = []
        self._t = 0.0

    def reset(self) -> None:
        self._deposits = []
        self._t = 0.0

    def q(self, age_s: float) -> float:
        if self.beta == 0.0:
            return 0.0
        return float(np.exp(-(max(float(age_s), 1e-12) / self.tau_s) ** self.beta))

    def step(self, drive):
        drive = np.asarray(drive)
        self._t += self.dt
        if drive.any():
            self._deposits.append((self._t, drive * self.dt))
        if self._deposits:
            cutoff = self._t - self.horizon_s
            self._deposits = [(t, v) for t, v in self._deposits if t > cutoff]
        total = np.zeros((self.B, self.S, self.A))
        for deposited_at, value in self._deposits:
            total += value * self.q(self._t - deposited_at)
        return np.clip(total, -1.0, 1.0)

@dataclass
class TrialBlockResult:
    rewards: np.ndarray
    final_weights: np.ndarray
    membrane_residuals: list = field(default_factory=list)
    clipped_fraction: float = 0.0
    labels: np.ndarray = None
    update_rms: float = 0.0
    eligibility_at_reward: np.ndarray = None
    mixture_error: float = 0.0

def run_trial_block(bank_factory, geometry: str, *, B: int, trials: int, eta: float, eligibility_normalizer: float, streams: StreamBundle, w_max: float=W_MAX, w_init: float=W_INIT, forced_classes=None, forced_actions=None, record_membrane: bool=False, conflict: bool=True, conflict_shift_s: float=0.0, timing: dict | None=None) -> TrialBlockResult:
    if geometry not in GEOMETRIES:
        raise ValueError(f'unknown geometry {geometry!r}')
    timing = TIMING if timing is None else timing
    dt = timing['dt_s']
    reward_t = timing['reward_s']
    dur = timing['event_dur_s']
    old_t = timing['old_event_start_s']
    recent_t = timing['recent_event_start_s']
    if geometry == 'old_informative':
        schedule = [('informative', old_t), ('conflict', recent_t + conflict_shift_s)]
    else:
        schedule = [('conflict', old_t + conflict_shift_s), ('informative', recent_t)]
    if not conflict:
        schedule = [item for item in schedule if item[0] == 'informative']
    schedule.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    for (_, first), (_, second) in zip(schedule, schedule[1:]):
        if first + dur > second + 1e-09:
            raise ValueError('event windows overlap')
    if schedule[-1][1] + dur > reward_t + 1e-09:
        raise ValueError('event after reward')
    S, A = (2, 2)
    bank = bank_factory(B, S, A, dt)
    w = np.full((B, S, A), w_init)
    baseline = np.full(B, 1.0 / A)
    bidx = np.arange(B)
    rewards = np.zeros((B, trials))
    drive_buf = np.zeros((B, S, A))
    zero = np.zeros((B, S, A))
    thresh = DEVICE_CONDITIONS['in_rate'] * dt
    residuals, clipped, update_sq = ([], 0, [])
    label_log = np.zeros(trials, dtype=int)
    last_eligibility = None
    for trial in range(trials):
        bank.reset()
        if forced_classes is None:
            cls_ = streams.labels.integers(2, size=B)
        else:
            cls_ = forced_classes[trial].copy()
        label_log[trial] = int(cls_[0])
        spk = np.zeros((B, A))
        tnow = 0.0
        v = np.zeros((B, A))
        first_window = True
        previous_end = 0.0
        for role, onset in schedule:
            _relax(bank, int(round((onset - tnow) / dt)), dt)
            tnow = onset
            informative = role == 'informative'
            line = cls_ if informative else 1 - cls_
            if first_window:
                v = np.zeros((B, A))
                first_window = False
            else:
                v = v * np.exp(-(onset - previous_end) / DEVICE_CONDITIONS['tau_m'])
                if record_membrane:
                    residuals.append(float(np.abs(v).max()))
            wc = w[bidx, line]
            for _ in range(int(round(dur / dt))):
                pv = (streams.events.random(B) < thresh).astype(float)
                charge = wc * pv[:, None]
                v, sp = lif_step_batched(v, charge, dt, streams.explore, tau_m=DEVICE_CONDITIONS['tau_m'], v_th=DEVICE_CONDITIONS['v_th'], noise=DEVICE_CONDITIONS['sigma'])
                if informative:
                    spk += sp
                drive_buf[:] = 0.0
                drive_buf[bidx, line] = pv[:, None] * np.where(sp, 1.0, -DEVICE_CONDITIONS['ltd'])
                bank.step(drive_buf)
            tnow += dur
            previous_end = tnow
        e_rew = _relax(bank, int(round((reward_t - tnow) / dt)), dt)
        last_eligibility = np.array(e_rew, copy=True)
        if forced_actions is None:
            tie = spk.max(1) == spk.min(1)
            chosen = np.argmax(spk, 1)
            chosen[tie] = streams.explore.integers(A, size=int(tie.sum()))
        else:
            chosen = forced_actions[trial].copy()
        reward = (chosen == cls_).astype(float)
        raw = (reward - baseline)[:, None, None] * e_rew
        update_sq.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(raw ** 2))))
        proposed = w + eta * raw / eligibility_normalizer
        clipped += int(np.count_nonzero((proposed < 0.0) | (proposed > w_max)))
        w = np.clip(proposed, 0.0, w_max)
        baseline += 0.02 * (reward - baseline)
        rewards[:, trial] = reward
    return TrialBlockResult(rewards=rewards, final_weights=w, membrane_residuals=residuals, clipped_fraction=clipped / max(B * S * A * trials, 1), labels=label_log, update_rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(update_sq)))), eligibility_at_reward=last_eligibility, mixture_error=float(getattr(bank, 'fit_error', 0.0)))
DECLARED = {'lanes': 16, 'trials': 500}
ETA = 0.001
WINDOW_ARMS = {'full_226_full_duration': {'fit': (226.0, 'full_duration_floor')}, 'full_226_window_tail': {'fit': (226.0, 'window_tail_floor')}, 'short_30_window_tail': {'fit': (30.0, 'window_tail_floor')}, 'frozen_exponential': {'tau_s': 19.861465696861224, 'beta': 1.0}, 'no_trace': {'tau_s': 1.0, 'beta': 0.0}}
ARM_NORMALIZERS = {'full_226_full_duration': 0.007740341949100879, 'full_226_window_tail': 0.007743578407550522, 'short_30_window_tail': 0.0077999064202018764, 'frozen_exponential': 0.009158876820264537, 'no_trace': 1.0}
DECLARED_WEIGHTS = {'w_init': 0.7, 'w_max': 1.5}
ENDPOINTS = {'primary': {'name': 'I_window', 'upper': 'short_30_window_tail', 'lower': 'full_226_window_tail', 'predicted': {'h1': '+', 'h2': '+'}}, 'secondary': {'name': 'I_frozen_exponential', 'upper': 'full_226_full_duration', 'lower': 'frozen_exponential', 'predicted': {'h1': '+', 'h2': '-'}}}
GATE_TIERS = {'structural': tuple(WINDOW_ARMS), 'physical': tuple(WINDOW_ARMS), 'primary_behavioural': ('short_30_window_tail', 'full_226_window_tail'), 'secondary_behavioural': ('frozen_exponential',), 'control': ('no_trace',)}
HEADROOM_BAND = (0.55, 0.97)
CLIPPING_LIMIT = 0.05
CHANCE_BAND = (0.45, 0.55)

def sign_flip_floor(lanes: int) -> float:
    return 2.0 / 2 ** lanes

def arms_from_fits() -> dict:
    from .archives import load
    with load('device') as bundle:
        window = bundle['fit_window_s']
        convention = np.asarray(bundle['fit_convention'], dtype=str)
        tau, beta = (bundle['fit_tau_s'], bundle['fit_beta'])
        out = {}
        for arm, spec in WINDOW_ARMS.items():
            if 'fit' not in spec:
                out[arm] = {'tau_s': spec['tau_s'], 'beta': spec['beta']}
                continue
            want_window, want_convention = spec['fit']
            mask = (window == want_window) & (convention == want_convention)
            if not mask.any():
                raise LookupError(f"no fit rows for {arm!r} at {spec['fit']}")
            out[arm] = {'tau_s': float(tau[mask].mean()), 'beta': float(beta[mask].mean())}
    return out

def exact_bank(spec: dict):
    return lambda B, S, A, dt: ExactKernel(B, S, A, tau_s=spec['tau_s'], beta=spec['beta'], dt=dt, horizon_s=60.0)

def run_role_swap(*, lanes: int, trials: int, stream: str, progress=None) -> dict:
    if trials < 2 or trials % 2:
        raise ValueError('trials must be an even integer of at least two')
    budget = {'lanes': int(lanes), 'trials': int(trials)}
    arms = arms_from_fits()
    rows, cells = ([], {})
    for geometry in EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS:
        timing = evaluation_timing_for(geometry)
        for arm, spec in arms.items():
            if progress is not None:
                progress(f'{geometry}/{arm}')
            eta = 0.0 if spec['beta'] == 0.0 else ETA
            for role in GEOMETRIES:
                result = run_trial_block(exact_bank(spec), role, B=budget['lanes'], trials=budget['trials'], eta=eta, eligibility_normalizer=ARM_NORMALIZERS[arm], streams=named_streams(role, stream), timing=timing, record_membrane=True, **DECLARED_WEIGHTS)
                aulc = result.rewards.mean(axis=1)
                final = result.rewards[:, -budget['trials'] // 10:].mean(axis=1)
                for lane in range(budget['lanes']):
                    rows.append({'geometry': geometry, 'description': arm, 'role': role, 'run': lane, 'aulc': float(aulc[lane]), 'final_reward': float(final[lane]), 'clipped_fraction': float(result.clipped_fraction)})
                cells[arm, geometry, role] = {'aulc': aulc, 'final_mean': float(final.mean()), 'clipped_fraction': float(result.clipped_fraction), 'membrane_residual_max': max(result.membrane_residuals) if result.membrane_residuals else 0.0, 'realisation_error': float(result.mixture_error), 'labels': tuple((int(v) for v in np.asarray(result.labels)))}
    return {'rows': rows, 'gates': role_swap_gates(cells, budget), 'endpoints': role_swap_endpoints(cells, budget), 'protocol': {'lanes': budget['lanes'], 'trials': budget['trials'], 'eta': ETA, 'weights': dict(DECLARED_WEIGHTS), 'normalisers': dict(ARM_NORMALIZERS), 'arms': {a: dict(s) for a, s in arms.items()}}}

def role_swap_gates(cells, budget) -> dict:
    geometries = tuple(EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS)
    roles = GEOMETRIES
    labels = {}
    for (arm, geometry, role), block in cells.items():
        labels.setdefault((geometry, role), set()).add(block['labels'])
    expected = len(WINDOW_ARMS) * len(geometries) * len(roles)
    structural = {'complete_paired_cells': len(cells) == expected, 'label_identity_across_arms': all((len(v) == 1 for v in labels.values()))}
    physical = {'exact_kernels_only': all((b['realisation_error'] == 0.0 for b in cells.values())), 'membrane_continuity': all((b['membrane_residual_max'] <= MEMBRANE_RESIDUAL_TOLERANCE for b in cells.values()))}
    control = {'no_trace_at_chance': all((CHANCE_BAND[0] <= cells['no_trace', g, r]['final_mean'] <= CHANCE_BAND[1] for g in geometries for r in roles))}

    def behavioural(arms):
        return {'headroom': all((HEADROOM_BAND[0] <= cells[a, g, r]['final_mean'] <= HEADROOM_BAND[1] for a in arms for g in geometries for r in roles)), 'clipping': all((cells[a, g, r]['clipped_fraction'] <= CLIPPING_LIMIT for a in arms for g in geometries for r in roles))}
    primary = behavioural(GATE_TIERS['primary_behavioural'])
    secondary = behavioural(GATE_TIERS['secondary_behavioural'])
    active = tuple((a for a in WINDOW_ARMS if a != 'no_trace'))
    diagnostic = behavioural(active)
    run_is_valid = bool(all(structural.values()) and all(physical.values()) and all(control.values()) and all(primary.values()))
    return {'structural': structural, 'physical': physical, 'control': control, 'primary_behavioural': primary, 'secondary_behavioural': secondary, 'run_is_valid': run_is_valid, 'secondary_is_reportable': bool(run_is_valid and all(secondary.values())), 'tier_arm_sets': {k: list(v) for k, v in GATE_TIERS.items()}, 'non_binding_diagnostic': {'arms': list(active), **diagnostic, 'note': 'reported only; excluded from run_is_valid by design'}}

def role_swap_endpoints(cells, budget) -> dict:
    from .statistics import exact_sign_flip_p
    g = {(arm, geometry): cells[arm, geometry, 'recent_informative']['aulc'] - cells[arm, geometry, 'old_informative']['aulc'] for arm in WINDOW_ARMS for geometry in EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS}
    out = {}
    for family, block in ENDPOINTS.items():
        per_geometry = {}
        for geometry in EVALUATION_AGE_PAIRS:
            values = g[block['upper'], geometry] - g[block['lower'], geometry]
            observed = '+' if values.mean() > 0 else '-'
            per_geometry[geometry] = {'mean': float(values.mean()), 'sign_flip_p_two_sided': float(exact_sign_flip_p(list(values))), 'sign_flip_floor': sign_flip_floor(budget['lanes']), 'predicted_sign': block['predicted'][geometry], 'observed_sign': observed, 'sign_matches': block['predicted'][geometry] == observed}
        out[family] = {'endpoint': block['name'], 'upper_arm': block['upper'], 'lower_arm': block['lower'], 'per_geometry': per_geometry}
    return out
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/statistics.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/statistics.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import itertools
import math
import numpy as np
__all__ = ['mean_and_standard_error', 'exact_sign_flip_p', 'holm', 'bootstrap_ci', 'percentile_interval', 'interval_verdict']

def mean_and_standard_error(values):
    n = len(values)
    if n < 2:
        raise ValueError('a standard error needs at least two values')
    mean = math.fsum(values) / n
    variance = math.fsum(((value - mean) ** 2 for value in values)) / (n - 1)
    return (mean, math.sqrt(variance / n))

def exact_sign_flip_p(differences):
    n = len(differences)
    if n > 22:
        raise ValueError(f'enumeration over 2**{n} is not the right tool at this size')
    observed = abs(math.fsum(differences))
    hits = 0
    for signs in itertools.product((1.0, -1.0), repeat=n):
        total = math.fsum((sign * value for sign, value in zip(signs, differences)))
        if abs(total) >= observed - 1e-15:
            hits += 1
    return hits / 2 ** n

def holm(p_values):
    p_values = list(p_values)
    order = sorted(range(len(p_values)), key=lambda i: p_values[i])
    n, adjusted, running = (len(p_values), [0.0] * len(p_values), 0.0)
    for rank, index in enumerate(order):
        running = max(running, min(1.0, (n - rank) * p_values[index]))
        adjusted[index] = running
    return adjusted

def percentile_interval(values, *, n_boot=10000, seed=0, ci=95):
    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=float).ravel()
    if v.size == 0:
        return (float('nan'), (float('nan'), float('nan')))
    if v.size == 1:
        return (float(v[0]), (float(v[0]), float(v[0])))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    means = v[rng.integers(0, v.size, size=(n_boot, v.size))].mean(axis=1)
    half = (100 - ci) / 2
    return (float(v.mean()), (float(np.percentile(means, half)), float(np.percentile(means, 100 - half))))

def bootstrap_ci(values, n_boot=10000, ci=95, seed=0):
    return percentile_interval(values, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed, ci=ci)[1]

def interval_verdict(ci95, *, favourable='device_advantage', adverse='comparator_advantage', inconclusive='unresolved'):
    low, high = (float(ci95[0]), float(ci95[1]))
    if low > 0.0:
        return favourable
    if high < 0.0:
        return adverse
    return inconclusive
\end{Verbatim}

from __future__ import annotations
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
from matplotlib.colors import LinearSegmentedColormap
from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch, FancyBboxPatch
from mrl_trace.kernels import scalar_insufficiency_witness
from .data import role_swap_cells
from .figstyle import PALETTE, apply_style
DEVICE, SHORT, REF = (PALETTE['device'], PALETTE['linear'], PALETTE['reference'])
INK, GRID, GREY = (PALETTE['ink'], PALETTE['grid'], PALETTE['control'])
FIGSIZE = (7.0, 5.15)
TITLE, LABEL, TICK, NOTE = (8.2, 7.6, 7.4, 7.4)
DASH = (0, (2.6, 1.5))
TRIALS, BINS, CHANCE = (500, 10, 0.5)
SCHEMATIC_CELL = 'full_226_full_duration|h1'
FULL, WIN30 = ((16.01, 0.622), (9.54, 1.074))
ITO_TAU, ITO_BETA = (16.01085496325088, 0.6215469546368932)
TAU_E = 19.861465696861224
SIGN_CMAP = LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list('ito_vs_exp', [(0.0, PALETTE['exponential']), (0.5, '#ffffff'), (0.78, PALETTE['device']), (1.0, PALETTE['gold'])])
CALIB = ((1.0, 10.0), (3.0, 20.0))
EVAL = ((1.5, 11.0), (3.5, 19.0))
REC, OLD, DUR = (1.0, 10.0, 0.3)
kww = lambda t, p: np.exp(-(np.maximum(t, 0.0) / p[0]) ** p[1])

def resp(p, a):
    return float(kww(np.linspace(max(a - DUR, 0.0), a, 80), p).mean())

def stage(ax, letter, title, x0=0.0):
    ax.set_axis_off()
    ax.text(x0, 0.99, '%s.' % letter, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=TITLE, fontweight='bold', va='bottom', color=INK)

def frame(inner, *, ticks=False):
    for s in inner.spines.values():
        s.set_linewidth(0.7)
        s.set_color(GRID)
    if not ticks:
        inner.set_xticks([])
        inner.set_yticks([])

def panel_fit(ax):
    stage(ax, 'a', 'one record, two kernels', x0=0.15)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.135, 0.2, 0.855, 0.76])
    t = np.linspace(0.02, 30, 400)
    pl.plot(t, np.exp(-(t / ITO_TAU) ** ITO_BETA), '-', lw=2.0, color=DEVICE, zorder=4, label='measured SiO$_x$ kernel')
    pl.plot(t, np.exp(-t / TAU_E), '--', lw=2.0, color=PALETTE['exponential'], zorder=4, label='exponential control')
    q_ito = lambda d: float(np.exp(-(d / ITO_TAU) ** ITO_BETA))
    for d in sorted(CALIB[0] + CALIB[1]):
        pl.plot([d, d], [0.0, 1.0], lw=0.7, ls=':', color=GREY, zorder=1)
        pl.plot([d], [np.exp(-d / TAU_E)], 'o', ms=3.4, mfc='white', mec=PALETTE['exponential'], mew=1.1, zorder=6)
        pl.plot([d], [q_ito(d)], 'o', ms=3.4, mfc='white', mec=DEVICE, mew=1.1, zorder=6)
    a_, b_ = CALIB[0]
    q_exp = lambda d: float(np.exp(-d / TAU_E))
    for q_of, colour, x_arrow, value in ((q_exp, PALETTE['exponential'], 23.0, 1.573), (q_ito, DEVICE, 27.5, 1.765)):
        hi, lo = (q_of(a_), q_of(b_))
        for x_from, y in ((a_, hi), (b_, lo)):
            pl.plot([x_from, x_arrow], [y, y], lw=0.6, ls=(0, (2.0, 1.6)), color=colour, zorder=2)
        pl.annotate('', xy=(x_arrow, hi), xytext=(x_arrow, lo), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='<->', color=colour, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
        pl.text(x_arrow - 0.7, 0.5 * (hi + lo), '%.3f' % value, ha='right', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 1.8, color=colour)
    pl.set_xlim(0, 30)
    pl.set_ylim(0, 1.05)
    pl.set_xticks([0, 1, 3, 10, 20, 30])
    pl.set_xlabel('delay (s)', fontsize=LABEL - 0.6, labelpad=1)
    pl.set_ylabel('$q(\\ell)$', fontsize=LABEL, labelpad=2)
    pl.set_yticks([0, 0.5, 1.0])
    pl.set_yticklabels(['0', '0.5', '1.0'], fontsize=TICK - 1.0)
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    pl.tick_params(length=2)
    cal = plt.Line2D([], [], ls='none', marker='o', ms=3.4, mfc='white', mec=PALETTE['exponential'], mew=1.1)
    h, l = pl.get_legend_handles_labels()
    pl.legend(h + [cal], l + ['calibration delays'], frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.4, loc='lower left', handlelength=1.1, labelspacing=0.16, borderaxespad=0.15)
WITNESS_RETENTION_S = 23.043258124936226
WORKED_RATIO = 1.6

def panel_witness(ax):
    stage(ax, 'c', '', x0=0.185)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.165, 0.2, 0.82, 0.76])
    witness = scalar_insufficiency_witness(WITNESS_RETENTION_S)
    rows = witness['kernels']
    betas = [r['beta'] for r in rows]
    thresholds = [r['credit_threshold'] for r in rows]
    low, high = witness['disagreement_interval']
    assert low < WORKED_RATIO < high, 'the worked ratio must lie inside the interval it illustrates'
    interval_colour = PALETTE['rose']
    pl.plot(betas, thresholds, marker='o', ms=3.4, lw=1.8, color=DEVICE, zorder=4)
    span = high - low
    x0, x1 = (min(betas), max(betas))
    pad = 0.06 * (x1 - x0)
    pl.set_xlim(x0 - pad, 1.6)
    for level in (low, high):
        pl.plot([x0 - pad, x1 + 3.4 * pad], [level, level], lw=0.7, ls=(0, (3, 2)), color=interval_colour, zorder=2)
    xa = x1 + 3.0 * pad
    pl.annotate('', xy=(xa, high), xytext=(xa, low), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='<|-|>', color=interval_colour, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=6.0), zorder=5)
    pl.annotate('%.3f' % span, (xa, 0.5 * (low + high)), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(3, 0), va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 1.8, color=interval_colour, zorder=6)
    pl.plot([x0 - pad, x1 + 1.25 * pad], [WORKED_RATIO, WORKED_RATIO], lw=0.9, ls=(0, (5, 2)), color=INK, zorder=3)
    for label, dy in (('newer', 3.5), ('older', -8.0)):
        pl.annotate(label, (x1 + 0.6 * pad, WORKED_RATIO), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(0, dy), ha='center', fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, color=INK)
    pl.set_xlabel('$\\beta$', fontsize=LABEL, labelpad=1)
    pl.set_ylabel('$r^{\\ast}$', fontsize=LABEL, labelpad=2)
    pl.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 1.0, length=2.2, pad=1.5)
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    pl.grid(True, lw=0.4, color=GRID, alpha=0.7)
    pl.set_axisbelow(True)

def panel_predict(ax):
    stage(ax, 'b', '', x0=0.18)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.155, 0.17, 0.66, 0.73])
    tn = np.linspace(1.0, 3.5, 220)
    to = np.linspace(10.0, 20.0, 220)
    TN, TO = np.meshgrid(tn, to)
    q_ito = lambda t: np.exp(-(t / ITO_TAU) ** ITO_BETA)
    q_exp = lambda t: np.exp(-t / TAU_E)
    z = np.log(q_ito(TN) / q_ito(TO) / (q_exp(TN) / q_exp(TO)))
    lim = float(np.abs(z).max())
    mesh = pl.pcolormesh(TN, TO, z, cmap=SIGN_CMAP, vmin=-lim, vmax=lim, shading='auto', zorder=1)
    pl.contour(TN, TO, z, levels=[0.0], colors=[INK], linewidths=1.3, zorder=3)
    marks = []
    for (a_, b_), mk, lab in ((CALIB, 'P', 'choose $\\tau_{r^{*}}$'), (EVAL, 'D', 'evaluate learning')):
        h, = pl.plot([a_[0], b_[0]], [a_[1], b_[1]], mk, ms=5.6, mfc=INK, mec='white', mew=1.0, zorder=4, ls='none')
        marks.append((h, lab))
    pl.legend([h for h, _ in marks], [l for _, l in marks], frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, loc='lower center', bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.005), ncol=2, handlelength=1.0, handletextpad=0.4, columnspacing=1.2, borderaxespad=0.0)
    pl.set_xlabel('newer-event delay (s)', fontsize=LABEL - 0.6, labelpad=1)
    pl.set_ylabel('older-event delay (s)', fontsize=LABEL - 0.6, labelpad=2)
    pl.set_xlim(0.88, 3.62)
    pl.set_ylim(9.5, 20.5)
    pl.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 1.0, length=2)
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    cax = ax.inset_axes([0.845, 0.17, 0.038, 0.73])
    cb = ax.get_figure().colorbar(mesh, cax=cax, ticks=[-lim, 0.0, lim])
    cb.ax.set_yticklabels(['control', 'equal', 'SiO$_x$'], fontsize=NOTE - 1.4)
    cb.ax.invert_yaxis()
    cb.ax.tick_params(length=2, pad=2)
    cb.outline.set(linewidth=0.7, edgecolor=GRID)

def panel_action(ax):
    stage(ax, 'b', '', x0=0.145)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.145, 0.1, 0.84, 0.82])
    Y_PI, Y_DI = (0.905, 0.78)
    Y, Y_TGT = ({1: 0.655, 0: 0.53}, 0.385)

    X_GUIDE = 0.095
    X_END = 0.383
    X_FWD = 0.47
