    X_JOIN, X_APEX, Y_APEX = (0.735, 0.79, 0.489)
    pl.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((INFO[0], 0.492), INFO[1] - INFO[0], 0.478, facecolor=DEVICE, alpha=0.13, lw=0, zorder=1))
    pl.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((CONF[0], 0.492), CONF[1] - CONF[0], 0.478, facecolor=GREY, alpha=0.13, lw=0, zorder=1))
    pl.text(np.mean(INFO), 1.0, 'predictor', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE - 2.6, color=DEVICE)
    pl.text(np.mean(CONF), 1.0, 'competitor', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE - 2.6, color=GREY)
    IN_I = (0.027, 0.061, 0.095, 0.147, 0.179, 0.207)
    IN_C = (0.263, 0.295, 0.327, 0.357)
    OUT = {(1, 'i'): (0.03, 0.064, 0.098, 0.15, 0.182, 0.21), (0, 'i'): (0.03, 0.15, 0.21), (1, 'c'): (0.266, 0.298, 0.33, 0.358), (0, 'c'): (0.268, 0.33)}
    for row_y, xs, col in ((Y_PI, IN_I, DEVICE), (Y_DI, IN_C, GREY)):
        for x in xs:
            pl.plot([x, x], [row_y - 0.04, row_y + 0.04], lw=0.9, color=col, zorder=4)
    for (n, w), xs in OUT.items():
        col = DEVICE if w == 'i' else GREY
        for x in xs:
            pl.plot([x, x], [Y[n] - 0.034, Y[n] + 0.034], lw=0.9, color=col, alpha=1.0 if w == 'i' else 0.55, zorder=4)
    for row_y in (Y_PI, Y_DI):
        pl.plot([0.002, X_END], [row_y, row_y], lw=0.7, color=GRID, zorder=1)
    pl.plot([0.002, X_END], [Y[1], Y[1]], lw=2.4, color=GRID, zorder=1)
    pl.plot([0.002, X_END], [Y[0], Y[0]], lw=0.8, color=GRID, zorder=1)
    pl.plot([X_GUIDE, X_GUIDE], [Y[0] - 0.042, Y_PI + 0.046], lw=0.7, ls=(0, (2.2, 1.6)), color=INK, zorder=5)
    pl.text(X_GUIDE, Y[1] + 0.048, '$+1$', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.4, color=INK, zorder=7, bbox=dict(boxstyle='square,pad=0.08', fc='white', ec='none'))
    pl.text(X_GUIDE, Y[0] + 0.048, '$-0.6$', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.4, color=INK, zorder=7, bbox=dict(boxstyle='square,pad=0.08', fc='white', ec='none'))
    HALF, Y_DIM = (0.017, 0.462)
    pl.plot([X_GUIDE - HALF, X_GUIDE + HALF], [Y_DIM, Y_DIM], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=5)
    for xe in (X_GUIDE - HALF, X_GUIDE + HALF):
        pl.plot([xe, xe], [Y_DIM, Y_DIM + 0.016], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=5)
    pl.text(X_GUIDE, Y_DIM - 0.008, '5 ms', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE - 2.4, color=INK)
    for n in (1, 0):
        pl.plot([X_END, X_FWD], [Y[n], Y[n]], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
        pl.plot([X_FWD, X_A1], [Y[n], Y_A1], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
    pl.plot([X_A1, X_JOIN], [Y_A1, Y_A1], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
    pl.text(0.5 * (X_A1 + X_JOIN), Y_A1 + 0.028, 'action = 1', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=NOTE - 1.8, color=INK)
    pl.plot([0.002, X_JOIN], [Y_TGT, Y_TGT], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
    pl.plot([X_JOIN, X_APEX], [Y_TGT, Y_APEX], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
    pl.plot([X_JOIN, X_APEX], [Y_A1, Y_APEX], lw=0.8, color=INK, zorder=3)
    pl.annotate('', xy=(0.831, Y_APEX), xytext=(X_APEX, Y_APEX), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-|>', color=INK, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
    pl.text(0.841, Y_APEX, 'match', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.6, color=INK)
    pl.annotate('', xy=(0.96, Y_APEX), xytext=(0.93, Y_APEX), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-|>', color=INK, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
    pl.text(0.97, Y_APEX, '$R_n\\!=\\!1$', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.6, color=REF)
    wk = [plt.Line2D([], [], color=GRID, lw=2.4), plt.Line2D([], [], color=GRID, lw=0.8)]
    leg = pl.legend(wk, ['higher weight, more spikes', 'lower weight, fewer spikes'], title='$w_{ij}$', frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 2.6, loc='upper left', bbox_to_anchor=(0.52, 1.01), bbox_transform=pl.transData, handlelength=1.6, handletextpad=0.5, labelspacing=0.3, borderaxespad=0.0, alignment='left')
    leg.get_title().set_fontsize(NOTE - 2.6)
    pl.set_xlim(0, 1.0)
    pl.set_ylim(0.22, 1.02)
    pl.set_yticks([Y_TGT, Y[0], Y[1], Y_DI, Y_PI])
    pl.set_yticklabels(['trial target = 1', 'output 0', 'output 1', 'competitor\ninput', 'predictor\ninput'], fontsize=TICK - 1.8)
    pl.set_xticks([])
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    pl.tick_params(length=2, pad=1.5)
    for side in ('top', 'right', 'bottom'):
        pl.spines[side].set_visible(False)
    pl.grid(False)

def panel_update(ax):
    stage(ax, 'c', '', x0=0.02)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.02, 0.06, 0.96, 0.9])
    pl.set_axis_off()
    Y = {'info': 0.545, 'conf': 0.265}
    Y_MID = 0.405
    X_MUL = 0.322
    NODE_H = 0.085
    ASPECT = 2.15

    def node(x0, x1, y, colour, *, h=NODE_H, lw=0.8, alpha=0.13):
        pl.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch((x0, y - h), x1 - x0, 2 * h, boxstyle='round,pad=0,rounding_size=0.030', mutation_aspect=ASPECT, facecolor=colour, alpha=alpha, edgecolor=colour, lw=lw, zorder=3))
    pl.text(0.076, 0.855, '$R_n\\in\\{0,1\\}$', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.2, color=REF)
    pl.annotate('', xy=(0.262, 0.855), xytext=(0.226, 0.855), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-|>', color=REF, lw=0.9, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
    node(0.268, 0.503, 0.855, REF, h=0.085, lw=0.9, alpha=0.11)
    pl.text(0.385, 0.855, '$\\delta_n=R_n\\!-\\!b_n$', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.2, color=REF)
    for key, rad in (('info', -0.26), ('conf', -0.46)):
        pl.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch((0.345, 0.768), (X_MUL, Y[key]), arrowstyle='-', connectionstyle='arc3,rad=%.2f' % rad, color=REF, lw=1.0, zorder=2))
    pl.text(0.3, 0.66, 'shared', ha='right', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.4, color=REF)
    lag = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 60)
    glyph = np.exp(-(lag * 2.3) ** 0.62)
    for key, col, role in (('info', DEVICE, 'predictor'), ('conf', GREY, 'competitor')):
        node(0.016, 0.286, Y[key], col)
        gx = 0.032 + 0.06 * lag
        gy = Y[key] - 0.048 + 0.096 * glyph
        pl.fill_between(gx, Y[key] - 0.048, gy, color=col, alpha=0.45, lw=0, zorder=4)
        pl.plot(gx, gy, color=col, lw=1.0, zorder=5)
        pl.plot([gx[-1]], [gy[-1]], marker='o', ms=2.4, mfc=col, mec='white', mew=0.6, zorder=6)
        pl.text(0.198, Y[key], '$e_{\\mathrm{%s}}(t_R)$' % role, ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=INK, zorder=5)
        pl.annotate('', xy=(0.446, Y[key]), xytext=(0.294, Y[key]), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-|>', color=INK, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
        pl.text(X_MUL + 0.04, Y[key], '$\\times\\,\\eta\\delta_n$', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=REF, zorder=6, bbox=dict(boxstyle='square,pad=0.10', fc='white', ec='none'))
        node(0.454, 0.646, Y[key], col)
        pl.text(0.55, Y[key], '$\\Delta w_{\\mathrm{%s}}$' % role, ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=INK, zorder=5)
        pl.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch((0.654, Y[key]), (0.706, Y_MID), arrowstyle='-|>', connectionstyle='arc3,rad=%.2f' % (0.24 if key == 'conf' else -0.24), color=INK, lw=0.8, mutation_scale=5, zorder=3))
    node(0.712, 0.988, Y_MID, INK, alpha=0.07)
    pl.text(0.85, Y_MID, '$w_{ij}\\!\\leftarrow\\!w_{ij}\\!+\\!\\Delta w_{ij}$', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=INK, zorder=5)
    pl.annotate('', xy=(0.85, Y_MID - NODE_H - 0.105), xytext=(0.85, Y_MID - NODE_H - 0.012), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-|>', color=INK, lw=0.8, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=5))
    pl.text(0.85, Y_MID - NODE_H - 0.13, 'next trial', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=INK)
    pl.set_xlim(0, 1.0)
    pl.set_ylim(0.0, 1.0)

def panel_test(ax):
    stage(ax, 'a', '', x0=0.1)
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.1, 0.13, 0.885, 0.74])
    T_OLD, T_NEW, T_R, T_MAX = (0.35, 3.1, 4.4, 6.45)
    EV = 0.4
    DEPTH = 0.24
    OFF = (0.04, 0.14, 0.24, 0.35)
    INC, SCALE, BQ = (0.165, 1.9, 0.62)
    q = lambda age: np.exp(-(np.maximum(age, 0.0) / SCALE) ** BQ)
    for base, older_is_predictor in ((1.4, True), (0.42, False)):
        for t_e, is_older in ((T_OLD, True), (T_NEW, False)):
            col = DEVICE if is_older == older_is_predictor else GREY
            spikes = [t_e + d for d in OFF]
            for st in spikes:
                pl.plot([st, st], [base - DEPTH, base], lw=0.8, color=col, zorder=4)
            xs = np.linspace(t_e, T_R, 260)
            tot = np.zeros_like(xs)
            for st in spikes:
                tail = np.where(xs >= st, INC * q(xs - st), 0.0)
                tot = tot + tail
                pl.plot(xs, base + tail, lw=0.6, color=col, alpha=0.32, zorder=2)
            pl.plot(xs, base + tot, lw=1.5, color=col, zorder=5)
            pl.plot([T_R], [base + tot[-1]], marker='o', ms=3.4, mfc=col, mec='white', mew=0.8, zorder=6)
        pl.plot([0.0, T_R + 1.3], [base, base], color=GRID, lw=0.7, zorder=1)
    pl.axvline(T_R, color=REF, lw=1.1, ls='--', zorder=5)
    pl.text(T_R - 0.12, 2.17, 'reward', ha='right', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 1.8, color=REF)
    pl.text(T_R + 0.12, 2.17, '$R_n\\in\\{0,1\\}$', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=REF)
    pl.set_xlim(0, T_MAX)
    pl.set_ylim(0.05, 2.3)
    pl.set_xticks([T_OLD + EV / 2, T_NEW + EV / 2])
    pl.set_xticklabels(['older', 'newer'], fontsize=TICK - 1.4)
    Y_TICKS = [0.42 - DEPTH / 2, 0.42 + 0.31, 1.4 - DEPTH / 2, 1.4 + 0.31]
    for yt in Y_TICKS:
        pl.plot([0.0, T_R + 1.3], [yt, yt], color=GRID, lw=0.7, zorder=1)
    pl.set_yticks(Y_TICKS)
    pl.set_yticklabels(['input\nspikes', 'sum', 'input\nspikes', 'sum'], fontsize=TICK - 1.8)
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    pl.tick_params(length=2, pad=1.5)
    pl.grid(False)
    handles = [plt.Line2D([], [], color=DEVICE, lw=1.5), plt.Line2D([], [], color=GREY, lw=1.5)]
    pl.legend(handles, ['$e_{\\mathrm{predictor}}$', '$e_{\\mathrm{competitor}}$'], frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, loc='lower center', bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.005), ncol=2, handlelength=1.2, handletextpad=0.4, columnspacing=0.8, borderaxespad=0.0)

def learning_curves():
    cells = role_swap_cells('full_226_full_duration', 'h1')
    n = np.arange(1, TRIALS + 1)
    tail = n > TRIALS - TRIALS // BINS
    out = {}
    for role in ('recent_informative', 'old_informative'):
        cell = cells[role]
        aulc, final = (cell['aulc_mean'], cell['final_reward_mean'])

        def state(n_c):
            e = np.exp(-n / n_c)
            r_inf = (aulc - CHANCE * e.mean()) / (1.0 - e.mean())
            return (r_inf, r_inf + (CHANCE - r_inf) * e[tail].mean())
        lo, hi = (2.0, 2000.0)
        assert (state(lo)[1] - final) * (state(hi)[1] - final) < 0, role
        for _ in range(200):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            if (state(lo)[1] - final) * (state(mid)[1] - final) <= 0:
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid
        n_c = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        r_inf = state(n_c)[0]
        curve = r_inf - (r_inf - CHANCE) * np.exp(-n / n_c)
        assert abs(curve.mean() - aulc) < 1e-09, (role, curve.mean(), aulc)
        assert abs(curve[tail].mean() - final) < 1e-09, (role, curve[tail].mean(), final)
        out[role] = dict(n=n, curve=curve, aulc=aulc, final=final)
    assert out['recent_informative']['aulc'] > out['old_informative']['aulc']
    return out

def panel_learning(ax):
    stage(ax, 'd', '', x0=0.135)
    D = learning_curves()
    n = D['recent_informative']['n']
    hi, lo = (D['recent_informative']['curve'], D['old_informative']['curve'])
    assert (hi > lo).all(), 'the newer-predictor curve must lie above the older one everywhere'
    pl = ax.inset_axes([0.135, 0.19, 0.85, 0.68])
    pl.fill_between(n, lo, hi, color=DEVICE, alpha=0.32, lw=0, zorder=2)
    pl.plot(n, hi, lw=1.5, color=DEVICE, zorder=4)
    pl.plot(n, lo, lw=1.5, color=DEVICE, ls=DASH, zorder=4)
    pl.axhline(CHANCE, color=INK, lw=0.8, ls='--', zorder=3)
    handles = [plt.Line2D([], [], color=DEVICE, lw=1.5), plt.Line2D([], [], color=DEVICE, lw=1.5, ls=DASH), plt.Rectangle((0, 0), 1, 1, facecolor=DEVICE, alpha=0.32, lw=0)]
    labels = ['newer predictor', 'older predictor', '$g=\\overline{R}_{\\mathrm{newer}}-\\overline{R}_{\\mathrm{older}}$']
    pl.legend(handles, labels, frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, loc='upper left', handlelength=2.2, handletextpad=0.5, labelspacing=0.28, borderaxespad=0.12)
    pl.text(TRIALS * 0.99, CHANCE + 0.018, 'chance', ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=NOTE - 2.2, color=INK)
    pl.set_xlim(1, TRIALS)
    pl.set_ylim(0.455, 1.1)
    pl.set_xticks([1, 250, TRIALS])
    pl.set_xticklabels(['1', '250', '500'], fontsize=TICK - 1.4)
    pl.set_yticks([CHANCE, 0.75, 1.0])
    pl.set_yticklabels(['0.5', '0.75', '1'], fontsize=TICK - 1.4)
    pl.set_xlabel('training trial', fontsize=LABEL - 1.2, labelpad=0)
    pl.set_ylabel('mean reward rate', fontsize=LABEL - 1.2, labelpad=2)
    pl.text(0.5, 1.035, 'both assignments, 500 trials', transform=pl.transAxes, ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=NOTE - 2.0, color=INK)
    frame(pl, ticks=True)
    pl.tick_params(length=2, pad=1.5)

def protocol_figure():
    apply_style()
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(7.0, 2.62), gridspec_kw={'wspace': 0.055})
    panel_fit(axes[0])
    panel_predict(axes[1])
    panel_witness(axes[2])
    figure.subplots_adjust(left=0.012, right=0.988, top=0.94, bottom=0.06)
    return figure

def task_figure():
    apply_style()
    figure = plt.figure(figsize=(7.0, 5.15))
    grid = figure.add_gridspec(2, 2, wspace=0.06, hspace=0.06)
    panel_test(figure.add_subplot(grid[0, 0]))
    panel_action(figure.add_subplot(grid[0, 1]))
    panel_update(figure.add_subplot(grid[1, 0]))
    panel_learning(figure.add_subplot(grid[1, 1]))
    figure.subplots_adjust(left=0.012, right=0.988, top=0.955, bottom=0.045)
    return figure
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/data.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/data.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Small data adapters shared by the plotting functions."""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict

import numpy as np

from mrl_trace.archives import load


def device_arrays() -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    with load("device") as bundle:
        return {name: bundle[name].copy() for name in bundle.files}


def fit_rows(*, robust: bool = False) -> list[dict]:
    data = device_arrays()
    prefix = "robust_" if robust else "fit_"
    fields = {
        "run": f"{prefix}run",
        "window_s": f"{prefix}window_s",
        "convention": f"{prefix}convention",
        "tau_s": f"{prefix}tau_s",
        "beta": f"{prefix}beta",
        "r2": f"{prefix}r_squared",
    }
    if not robust:
        fields.update({
            "amplitude_a": "fit_amplitude_a",
            "floor_a": "fit_floor_a",
            "n_samples": "fit_n_samples",
        })
    return [
        {name: data[source][index].item() for name, source in fields.items()}
        for index in range(data[fields["run"]].size)
    ]


def reference_traces():
    data = device_arrays()
    accepted = data["run"][data["accepted"]]
    traces = []
    fitted = defaultdict(list)
    for run in accepted:
        trace = data["trace_run"] == run
        t = data["trace_time_s"][trace]
        current = data["trace_current_a"][trace]
        keep = t <= 226.0
        traces.append((t, current, keep))

        row = ((data["fit_run"] == run)
               & (data["fit_window_s"] == 226.0)
               & (data["fit_convention"] == "full_duration_floor"))
        for key, source in (
            ("tau", "fit_tau_s"),
            ("beta", "fit_beta"),
            ("A", "fit_amplitude_a"),
            ("floor", "fit_floor_a"),
        ):
            fitted[key].append(float(data[source][row][0]))
    return traces, {key: np.asarray(value) for key, value in fitted.items()}


def role_swap_effects() -> dict[tuple[str, str], dict[int, float]]:
    with load("credit") as bundle:
        cells: dict[tuple[str, str, int], dict[str, float]] = defaultdict(dict)
        for run, description, geometry, role, value in zip(
                bundle["run"], bundle["description"], bundle["geometry"],
                bundle["role"], bundle["aulc"]):
            cells[(str(description), str(geometry), int(run))][str(role)] = float(value)
    effects: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[int, float]] = defaultdict(dict)
    for (description, geometry, run), roles in cells.items():
        if len(roles) == 2:
            effects[(description, geometry)][run] = (
                roles["recent_informative"] - roles["old_informative"])
    return effects


def role_swap_cells(description: str, geometry: str) -> dict[str, dict[str, float]]:
    with load("credit") as bundle:
        mask = ((bundle["description"] == description)
                & (bundle["geometry"] == geometry))
        roles = np.asarray(bundle["role"][mask], str)
        aulc = bundle["aulc"][mask]
        final = bundle["final_reward"][mask]
    return {
        role: {
            "aulc_mean": float(aulc[roles == role].mean()),
            "final_reward_mean": float(final[roles == role].mean()),
        }
        for role in sorted(set(roles))
    }
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/external.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/external.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Fit-window sensitivity in the optional external dataset."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from mrl_trace.archives import external_cache
from . import figstyle

COMPLETE_WINDOW = "1.0000xrecord"
TRUNCATED_WINDOW = "0.1327xrecord"
CONVENTION = "free_floor"


def _load():
    path = external_cache()
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "prepare the external workbook with tools/prepare_data.py external WORKBOOK")
    with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as bundle:
        return {name: bundle[name].copy() for name in bundle.files}


def external_window_table() -> list[dict]:
    bundle = _load()
    return [
        {
            "record": str(record),
            "porosity_percent": float(porosity),
            "beta_complete": float(complete),
            "beta_truncated": float(truncated),
            "change": float(truncated - complete),
        }
        for record, porosity, complete, truncated in zip(
            bundle["trace_record"], bundle["trace_porosity_percent"],
            bundle["beta_complete"], bundle["beta_truncated"])
    ]


def _kww(t, floor, amplitude, tau, beta):
    return floor + amplitude * np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(t, 0.0) / tau, beta))


def window_sensitivity_external():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    figstyle.apply_style()
    bundle = _load()
    names = list(map(str, bundle["trace_record"]))
    colours = figstyle.sequential(len(names))
    complete = float(np.max(bundle["time_s"]))
    cut = complete * float(bundle["truncated_fraction"])
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 2.9))

    ax = axes[0]
    ax.axvspan(0.0, cut, color=figstyle.PALETTE["pale"], alpha=0.40, lw=0, zorder=0)
    records = np.asarray(bundle["record"], str)
    for index, (name, colour) in enumerate(zip(names, colours)):
        mask = records == name
        order = np.argsort(bundle["time_s"][mask])
        time = bundle["time_s"][mask][order]
        current = bundle["current_normalised"][mask][order]
        ax.plot(time, current, ".", ms=1.6, alpha=0.55, color=colour, zorder=2)
        ax.plot(
            time,
            _kww(time, bundle["floor_complete"][index],
                 bundle["amplitude_complete"][index],
                 bundle["tau_complete_s"][index],
                 bundle["beta_complete"][index]),
            lw=1.0, color=colour, zorder=3,
            label=f"{bundle['trace_porosity_percent'][index]:.1f}%")
    ax.set_xlabel("time after the drive (s)")
    ax.set_ylabel("current, normalised")
    ax.set_yscale("log")
    ax.text(cut / 2, 3e-4, f"truncated fit: 0 to {cut:.2f} s",
            rotation=90, ha="center", va="bottom", fontsize=5.6,
            color=figstyle.PALETTE["ink"], zorder=4)
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=5.6, title="porosity", title_fontsize=5.6,
              loc="upper left", bbox_to_anchor=(1.01, 1.02), handlelength=1.1,
              borderaxespad=0.0, labelspacing=0.35)
    figstyle.house_grid(ax)
    figstyle.panel(ax, "a")

    ax = axes[1]
    rows = external_window_table()
    positions = np.arange(len(rows))
    colour = figstyle.PALETTE["device"]
    for x, row in zip(positions, rows):
        ax.plot([x, x], [row["beta_complete"], row["beta_truncated"]],
                "-", lw=1.4, color=colour, zorder=2)
    ax.plot(positions, [row["beta_complete"] for row in rows], "o",
            ms=5.0, color=colour, zorder=3, label=f"complete, {complete:.1f}\u2009s")
    ax.plot(positions, [row["beta_truncated"] for row in rows], "s",
            ms=5.0, color=colour, zorder=3, label=f"truncated, {cut:.2f}\u2009s")
    if not all(row["change"] > 0 for row in rows):
        raise AssertionError("the exponent no longer rises in every external trace")
    ax.set_xticks(positions)
    ax.set_xticklabels([f"{row['porosity_percent']:.1f}" for row in rows], fontsize=6.4)
    ax.set_xlim(-0.55, len(rows) - 0.45)
    ax.set_xlabel(r"mesoporous SiO$_2$: porosity (%)")
    ax.set_ylabel(r"fitted dispersion exponent $\beta$")
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=6.0, loc="upper left",
              handlelength=1.0, handletextpad=0.4, labelspacing=0.3)
    figstyle.house_grid(ax)
    figstyle.panel(ax, "b")
    fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/figstyle.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/figstyle.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Compact plotting style shared by the release figures."""
from __future__ import annotations

PALETTE = {
    "device": "#22a884",
    "exponential": "#414487",
    "linear": "#297d8d",
    "control": "#9aa6b2",
    "reference": "#c0392b",
    "gold": "#9bd746",
    "ink": "#2b2b2b",
    "grid": "#c8d0d8",
    "violet": "#423078",
    "slate": "#7f7f7f",
    "rose": "#34618b",
    "pale": "#8d99a6",
    "reward": "#431563",
    "copper": "#6aca5a",
    "teal": "#46b96f",
    "guide": "#8a949f",
}

DEVICE = PALETTE["device"]
COMPARATOR = PALETTE["exponential"]
LINEAR = PALETTE["linear"]
CONTROL = PALETTE["control"]
REFERENCE = PALETTE["reference"]
INK = PALETTE["ink"]
GRID = PALETTE["grid"]
VIOLET = PALETTE["violet"]
REWARD = PALETTE["reward"]
GUIDE = PALETTE["guide"]


def apply_style() -> None:
    import matplotlib as mpl

    mpl.rcParams.update({
        "figure.dpi": 120,
        "figure.facecolor": "white",
        "savefig.dpi": 400,
        "savefig.facecolor": "white",
        "savefig.bbox": "tight",
        "savefig.pad_inches": 0.02,
        "font.family": "sans-serif",
        "font.sans-serif": ["DejaVu Sans", "Helvetica", "Arial", "sans-serif"],
        "mathtext.fontset": "dejavusans",
        "font.size": 7.8,
        "axes.labelsize": 7.8,
        "axes.titlesize": 8.6,
        "legend.fontsize": 7.4,
        "xtick.labelsize": 7.4,
        "ytick.labelsize": 7.4,
        "figure.titlesize": 8.6,
        "axes.titleweight": "normal",
        "axes.labelpad": 2.5,
        "xtick.major.pad": 2.0,
        "ytick.major.pad": 2.0,
        "xtick.major.size": 3.0,
        "ytick.major.size": 3.0,
        "xtick.minor.size": 1.8,
        "ytick.minor.size": 1.8,
        "axes.grid": True,
        "axes.axisbelow": True,
        "grid.color": PALETTE["grid"],
        "grid.linewidth": 0.5,
        "grid.alpha": 1.0,
        "grid.linestyle": "-",
        "axes.spines.top": False,
        "axes.spines.right": False,
        "axes.linewidth": 0.8,
        "lines.linewidth": 1.8,
        "lines.solid_capstyle": "round",
        "xtick.direction": "out",
        "ytick.direction": "out",
        "legend.frameon": False,
        "legend.borderaxespad": 0.4,
        "legend.handlelength": 1.6,
        "legend.handletextpad": 0.5,
        "legend.columnspacing": 1.0,
        "legend.labelspacing": 0.35,
    })


def house_grid(ax) -> None:
    ax.grid(True, color=PALETTE["grid"], linewidth=0.5, alpha=1.0)
    ax.set_axisbelow(True)


def panel(ax, letter: str) -> None:
    ax.set_title(str(letter).strip("(). "), loc="left", fontweight="bold", fontsize=8.6)
    house_grid(ax)


def sequential(count: int, low: float = 0.10, high: float = 0.85):
    import matplotlib.colors as colours
    import numpy as np

    ramp = colours.LinearSegmentedColormap.from_list(
        "mrl_fill",
        ["#440154", "#414487", "#2a788e", "#22a884", "#7ad151", "#fde725"])
    return [ramp(value) for value in np.linspace(low, min(high, 0.85), max(count, 1))]
\end{Verbatim}

\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/kernel\_origin.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/kernel\_origin.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch
from .figstyle import PALETTE
INK = PALETTE['ink']
CB = PALETTE['grid']
TRAP = PALETTE['device']
ELEC = PALETTE['device']
CAP = PALETTE['reference']
SPIKE = PALETTE['violet']
NOTATION = {
    'compact': {'tau': '\\tau', 'beta': '\\beta', 'lag': '\\ell'},
    'subscripted': {'tau': '\\tau_{leak}', 'beta': '\\beta_{leak}', 'lag': 'l'},
}
SYM = NOTATION['compact']
FIGSIZE = (7.3, 2.52)
WIDTH_RATIOS = (1.0, 1.0, 1.0)
HEIGHT_RATIOS = (1.0, 0.2)
LINE_SCALE = 0.62
LABEL_SIZE = 6.4
TICK_SIZE = 5.9
NOTE_SIZE = 6.1
LETTER_SIZE = 8.2

def thin(ax, factor: float=LINE_SCALE) -> None:
    for line in ax.lines:
        line.set_linewidth(line.get_linewidth() * factor)
        if line.get_markeredgewidth():
            line.set_markeredgewidth(line.get_markeredgewidth() * factor)
    for patch in ax.patches:
        if patch.get_linewidth():
            patch.set_linewidth(patch.get_linewidth() * factor)
    for artist in ax.texts:
        arrow = getattr(artist, 'arrow_patch', None)
        if arrow is not None:
            arrow.set_linewidth(arrow.get_linewidth() * factor)
            arrow.set_mutation_scale(arrow.get_mutation_scale() * 0.85)
    for spine in ax.spines.values():
        spine.set_linewidth(spine.get_linewidth() * factor)
    ax.tick_params(width=0.8 * factor)

def panel_a(ax):
    ax.set_xlim(0, 10)
    ax.axis('off')
    band_low, band_high = (8.4, 9.3)
    ax.fill_between([0.5, 9.5], band_low, band_high, color=CB, zorder=1)
    ax.plot([0.5, 9.5], [band_low, band_low], color=INK, lw=1.4, zorder=2)
    ax.text(0.7, (band_low + band_high) / 2, 'conduction band', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=NOTE_SIZE, color=INK)
    traps = [(2.0, 7.4), (4.3, 6.0), (6.6, 4.6)]
    for j, (x, y) in enumerate(traps):
        ax.plot([x - 0.55, x + 0.55], [y, y], color=TRAP, lw=2.4, zorder=3)
        ax.plot([x], [y + 0.2], marker='o', ms=5.0, mfc=ELEC, mec=INK, mew=0.8, zorder=5)
        ax.text(x, y - 0.58, f'$v_{{n{j + 1}}}$', ha='center', fontsize=NOTE_SIZE - 0.8, color=TRAP)
        if j < len(traps) - 1:
            xn, yn = traps[j + 1]
            ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch((x + 0.55, y), (xn - 0.55, yn), arrowstyle='-|>', mutation_scale=11, color=SPIKE, lw=1.5, connectionstyle='arc3,rad=-0.15', zorder=4))
    ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch((1.6, 8.3), (1.6, 7.42), arrowstyle='-|>', mutation_scale=11, color=SPIKE, lw=1.5, zorder=4))
    for x, y in traps:
        ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch((x + 0.45, y + 0.3), (x + 0.45, 8.3), arrowstyle='-|>', mutation_scale=9, color=CAP, lw=1.1, ls=(0, (2, 1.3)), zorder=4))
    ax.annotate('', xy=(8.5, 8.4), xytext=(8.5, 4.6), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='<->', color=INK, lw=1.0))
    ax.text(8.85, 6.4, '$E_T$ spread', fontsize=NOTE_SIZE - 0.8, color=INK, rotation=90, va='center')
    legend_y = band_high + 0.62
    first = ax.text(0.5, legend_y, 'capture', color=SPIKE, fontsize=NOTE_SIZE, ha='left')
    ax.figure.canvas.draw()
    renderer = ax.figure.canvas.get_renderer()
    right = ax.transData.inverted().transform((first.get_window_extent(renderer=renderer).x1, 0.0))[0]
    ax.text(right + 0.7, legend_y, 'emission', color=CAP, fontsize=NOTE_SIZE, ha='left')
    tops = [t.get_position()[1] for t in ax.texts]
    for line in ax.lines:
        tops.extend(line.get_ydata())
    ax.set_ylim(min(tops) - 2.0, max(tops) + 0.5)

def panel_b(ax):
    lag = np.linspace(0.0, 30.0, 600)
    TAU_FULL = 16.01
    for beta, colour, label in ((0.62, PALETTE['device'], f"${SYM['beta']}=0.62$"), (1.0, PALETTE['exponential'], f"${SYM['beta']}=1$"), (1.27, PALETTE['gold'], f"${SYM['beta']}=1.27$")):
        ax.plot(lag, np.exp(-(lag / TAU_FULL) ** beta), lw=2.4, color=colour, label=label)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 30)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.02)
    ax.set_xlabel(f"delay ${SYM['lag']}$ (s)", fontsize=LABEL_SIZE)
    ax.set_ylabel(f"surviving fraction $q({SYM['lag']})$", fontsize=LABEL_SIZE)
    leg = ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE_SIZE, loc='upper right', handlelength=1.2, labelspacing=0.28, borderaxespad=0.2, title=f"${SYM['tau']}=16$ s")
    leg.get_title().set_fontsize(NOTE_SIZE)
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK_SIZE)

def panel_c(ax):
    lag = np.linspace(0.0, 30.0, 600)
    BETA_FULL = 0.6215
    TAUS = (7.0, 12.0, 18.0)

    def shade(hex_colour, toward_white):
        r, g, b = (int(hex_colour[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (1, 3, 5))
        mix = lambda c: int(round(c + (255 - c) * toward_white))
        return '#%02x%02x%02x' % (mix(r), mix(g), mix(b))
    base = PALETTE['device']
    curves = list(zip(TAUS, (shade(base, 0.55), shade(base, 0.28), base)))
    for tau, colour in curves:
        ax.plot(lag, np.exp(-(lag / tau) ** BETA_FULL), lw=2.4, color=colour, label=f"${SYM['tau']}={tau:g}$ s")
    for delay in (3.0, 20.0):
        ax.axvline(delay, color=CAP, lw=1.0, ls='--', zorder=1)
        for tau, colour in curves:
            ax.plot([delay], [np.exp(-(delay / tau) ** BETA_FULL)], 'o', ms=3.6, color=colour, zorder=5, clip_on=False)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 30)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1.02)
    ax.set_xlabel(f"action-to-reward delay ${SYM['lag']}$ (s)", fontsize=LABEL_SIZE)
    ax.set_ylabel(f"eligibility $q({SYM['lag']})$", fontsize=LABEL_SIZE)
    leg = ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE_SIZE, loc='upper right', handlelength=1.2, labelspacing=0.28, borderaxespad=0.2, title=f"${SYM['beta']}=0.62$")
    leg.get_title().set_fontsize(NOTE_SIZE)
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK_SIZE)

def figure_compact():
    import matplotlib as mpl
    global SYM
    SYM = NOTATION['compact']
    with mpl.rc_context():
        mpl.rcdefaults()
        fig = plt.figure(figsize=(FIGSIZE[0] * 0.79, FIGSIZE[1]))
        grid = fig.add_gridspec(1, 2, wspace=0.34)
        axes = [fig.add_subplot(grid[0, 0]), fig.add_subplot(grid[0, 1])]
        panel_b(axes[0])
        panel_c(axes[1])
    for ax in axes:
        ax.set_axisbelow(True)
        ax.grid(True, color=PALETTE['grid'], linewidth=0.5, alpha=1.0, linestyle='-')
    for ax in axes:
        thin(ax)
    for ax, letter in zip(axes, 'ab'):
        for artist in list(ax.texts):
            if artist.get_text() in ('a', 'b', 'c'):
                artist.remove()
        ax.text(-0.08, 1.04, letter, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=LETTER_SIZE, fontweight='bold', va='bottom')
    fig._release_pad_inches = 0.1
    return fig

def figure_subscripted():
    import matplotlib as mpl
    global SYM
    SYM = NOTATION['subscripted']
    with mpl.rc_context():
        mpl.rcdefaults()
        fig = plt.figure(figsize=FIGSIZE)
        grid = fig.add_gridspec(2, 3, width_ratios=WIDTH_RATIOS, height_ratios=HEIGHT_RATIOS, wspace=0.34, hspace=0.0)
        axes = [fig.add_subplot(grid[:, 0]), fig.add_subplot(grid[0, 1]), fig.add_subplot(grid[0, 2])]
        panel_a(axes[0])
        panel_b(axes[1])
        panel_c(axes[2])
        axes[1].set_ylabel(f"trapped population $q({SYM['lag']})$", fontsize=LABEL_SIZE)
        for ax in axes[1:]:
            ax.set_xticks([0, 10, 20, 30])
    for ax in axes[1:]:
        ax.set_axisbelow(True)
        ax.grid(True, color=PALETTE['grid'], linewidth=0.5)
    for ax in axes:
        thin(ax)
    for ax, letter in zip(axes, 'abc'):
        ax.text(-0.08, 1.04, letter, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=LETTER_SIZE, fontweight='bold', va='bottom')
    fig._release_pad_inches = 0.1
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/learned.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/learned.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Timing-dependent learned credit."""
from __future__ import annotations

import warnings

from .data import role_swap_effects
from .figstyle import PALETTE, apply_style

ITO_ARM = "full_226_full_duration"
EXP_ARM = "frozen_exponential"
PAIRS = (("h1", r"$(1.5,\,11)$ s"), ("h2", r"$(3.5,\,19)$ s"))
DEVICE = PALETTE["device"]
CONTROL = PALETTE["exponential"]
INK = PALETTE["ink"]
LABEL, TICK, NOTE = 7.6, 7.4, 7.4


def box(ax, data, position, colour, *, vertical=True, width=0.46):
    result = ax.boxplot(
        [data], positions=[position], widths=width,
        orientation="vertical" if vertical else "horizontal", whis=(0, 100),
        showfliers=False, patch_artist=True, zorder=3)
    for patch in result["boxes"]:
        patch.set(facecolor=colour, alpha=0.30, edgecolor=colour, linewidth=1.3)
    for key in ("whiskers", "caps"):
        for artist in result[key]:
            artist.set(color=colour, linewidth=1.2)
    for artist in result["medians"]:
        artist.set(color=colour, linewidth=1.8)
    return result


def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    apply_style()
    effects = role_swap_effects()
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(
        1, 2, figsize=(7.0, 2.85),
        gridspec_kw={"width_ratios": (1.0, 1.05), "wspace": 0.28})

    ax = axes[0]
    positions = {
        ("h1", ITO_ARM): 1.0,
        ("h1", EXP_ARM): 1.85,
        ("h2", ITO_ARM): 3.15,
        ("h2", EXP_ARM): 4.0,
    }
    handles = []
    for pair, _ in PAIRS:
        runs = sorted(set(effects[(ITO_ARM, pair)]) & set(effects[(EXP_ARM, pair)]))
        for arm, colour in ((ITO_ARM, DEVICE), (EXP_ARM, CONTROL)):
            result = box(ax, [effects[(arm, pair)][run] for run in runs],
                         positions[(pair, arm)], colour)
            if pair == "h1":
                handles.append(result["boxes"][0])
    ax.set_xticks([1.425, 3.575])
    ax.set_xticklabels([label for _, label in PAIRS], fontsize=TICK)
    ax.set_xlim(0.5, 4.5)
    ax.set_ylabel("role-swap effect, $g$\n" r"(newer $-$ older predictor)",
                  fontsize=LABEL - 0.6)
    ax.set_xlabel("evaluation timing, newer/older", fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.legend(handles, [r"measured SiO$_x$ kernel", "exponential control"],
              frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.4, loc="upper left",
              handlelength=1.2, labelspacing=0.2, borderaxespad=0.12)
    ax.text(0.0, 1.03, "a", transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=8.2,
            fontweight="bold", va="bottom", color=INK)

    ax = axes[1]
    for index, (pair, _) in enumerate(PAIRS):
        runs = sorted(set(effects[(ITO_ARM, pair)]) & set(effects[(EXP_ARM, pair)]))
        differences = [
            effects[(ITO_ARM, pair)][run] - effects[(EXP_ARM, pair)][run]
            for run in runs
        ]
        mean = sum(differences) / len(differences)
        box(ax, differences, len(PAIRS) - 1 - index,
            DEVICE if mean > 0 else CONTROL, vertical=False, width=0.40)
    ax.axvline(0.0, color=INK, lw=1.0, ls="--", zorder=4)
    ax.set_yticks(range(len(PAIRS)))
    ax.set_yticklabels([label for _, label in PAIRS][::-1], fontsize=TICK)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.55, len(PAIRS) - 0.45)
    ax.set_xlabel(
        r"paired difference, $\Delta g=g(q_{\mathrm{SiO}_x})-g(q_{\mathrm{exp}})$",
        fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.set_xticks([-0.10, -0.05, 0.0, 0.05])
    ax.tick_params(axis="x", labelsize=TICK)
    ax.text(0.0, 1.03, "b", transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=8.2,
            fontweight="bold", va="bottom", color=INK)
    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.filterwarnings(
            "ignore", message="This figure includes Axes that are not compatible with tight_layout")
        fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/measured.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/measured.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Measured relaxation and aggregate kernel."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np

from .data import fit_rows, reference_traces
from .figstyle import PALETTE, apply_style, house_grid, panel

CANVAS = (7.05, 2.75)
LEGEND_PT = 7.4


def _kww(t, tau, beta):
    return np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(t, 1e-12) / tau, beta))


def primary_identification():
    rows = [row for row in fit_rows()
            if row["window_s"] == 226.0
            and row["convention"] == "full_duration_floor"]
    return (
        float(np.mean([row["tau_s"] for row in rows])),
        float(np.mean([row["beta"] for row in rows])),
    )


def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    apply_style()
    traces, fits = reference_traces()
    tau_full, beta_full = primary_identification()
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=CANVAS)

    ax = axes[0]
    for index, (t, current, keep) in enumerate(traces):
        tau, beta = fits["tau"][index], fits["beta"][index]
        amplitude, floor = fits["A"][index], fits["floor"][index]
        ax.plot(t[keep], current[keep] * 1e12, lw=0.7,
                color=PALETTE["control"], alpha=0.85, zorder=2,
                label="measured" if index == 0 else None)
        ax.plot(t[keep], (floor + amplitude * _kww(t[keep], tau, beta)) * 1e12,
                lw=1.1, color=PALETTE["device"], zorder=3,
                label="fitted" if index == 0 else None)
    ax.set_xlabel("time after bias removal, $t$ (s)")
    ax.set_ylabel(r"current, $|I|$ (pA)")
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=LEGEND_PT, loc="upper right", handlelength=1.2)
    house_grid(ax)
    panel(ax, "a")

    ax = axes[1]
    grid = np.logspace(math.log10(0.3), math.log10(240.0), 400)
    for index, (t, current, keep) in enumerate(traces):
        q = (current[keep] - fits["floor"][index]) / fits["A"][index]
        ax.plot(t[keep], q, lw=0.7, color=PALETTE["control"], alpha=0.85,
                zorder=2, label="normalised repeats" if index == 0 else None)
    ax.plot(grid, _kww(grid, tau_full, beta_full), lw=2.0,
            color=PALETTE["device"], zorder=4, label="KWW fit")
    ax.set_xscale("log")
    ax.set_xlim(0.3, 240.0)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.15)
    ax.set_xlabel("time after bias removal, $t$ (s)")
    ax.set_ylabel(r"normalised decay, $q$")
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=LEGEND_PT, loc="lower left", handlelength=1.2)
    house_grid(ax)
    panel(ax, "b")

    fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/multilayer\_agent.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/multilayer\_agent.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch, Rectangle
from . import figstyle as S
LABEL, NOTE = (7.2, 7.2)
ARRAY_FILL = '#f2f4f6'
ROWS = (0.5, 0.61, 0.72)
ARRAY_TOP, ARRAY_BOTTOM = (0.8, 0.42)
RETURN_Y = 0.22

def _arrow(ax, a, b, colour, *, lw=0.9, ls='-', scale=6.0, style='-|>', rad=0.0, z=5):
    ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch(a, b, arrowstyle=style, mutation_scale=scale, lw=lw, color=colour, linestyle=ls, shrinkA=1.0, shrinkB=1.0, connectionstyle=f'arc3,rad={rad}', zorder=z))

def _units(ax, x, *, colour, size=7.0):
    ax.plot([x] * len(ROWS), list(ROWS), 'o', ms=size, markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=colour, markeredgewidth=0.9, zorder=6)

def _array(ax, x0, w, label):
    h = ARRAY_TOP - ARRAY_BOTTOM
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((x0, ARRAY_BOTTOM), w, h, facecolor=ARRAY_FILL, edgecolor=S.INK, lw=1.0, zorder=3))
    for k in (1, 2):
        ax.plot([x0 + k * w / 3] * 2, [ARRAY_BOTTOM, ARRAY_TOP], '-', lw=0.5, color=S.CONTROL, zorder=4)
        ax.plot([x0, x0 + w], [ARRAY_BOTTOM + k * h / 3] * 2, '-', lw=0.5, color=S.CONTROL, zorder=4)
    for i in range(3):
        for j in range(3):
            ax.plot([x0 + (i + 0.5) * w / 3], [ARRAY_BOTTOM + (j + 0.5) * h / 3], 'o', ms=2.6, color=S.DEVICE, zorder=6)
    ax.text(x0 + w / 2, 0.37, label, ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)

def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    S.apply_style()
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(6.05, 2.1))
    ax = fig.add_axes([0.01, 0.02, 0.98, 0.96])
    ax.set_xlim(0, 0.875)
    ax.set_ylim(0.1, 1.0)
    ax.set_axis_off()
    _units(ax, 0.035, colour=S.CONTROL, size=6.0)
    ax.text(0.035, 0.37, 'state', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    _array(ax, 0.1, 0.18, '$W_1$')
    _units(ax, 0.375, colour=S.INK)
    ax.text(0.352, 0.37, 'hidden', ha='right', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    _array(ax, 0.47, 0.18, '$W_2$')
    _units(ax, 0.745, colour=S.INK)
    ax.text(0.79, 0.61, 'action', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    for y in ROWS:
        _arrow(ax, (0.055, y), (0.095, y), S.CONTROL, lw=0.7, scale=4.5)
        _arrow(ax, (0.285, y), (0.357, y), S.INK, lw=0.7, scale=4.5)
        _arrow(ax, (0.395, y), (0.465, y), S.INK, lw=0.7, scale=4.5)
        _arrow(ax, (0.655, y), (0.727, y), S.INK, lw=0.7, scale=4.5)
    ax.plot([0.1, 0.65], [0.93, 0.93], '-', lw=1.2, color=S.REFERENCE, zorder=2)
    for x in (0.19, 0.56):
        _arrow(ax, (x, 0.93), (x, ARRAY_TOP + 0.015), S.REFERENCE, lw=0.8, ls=(0, (2.0, 1.4)), scale=5.5)
    ax.text(0.665, 0.93, 'reward $(R-b)$, one wire', ha='left', va='center', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.REFERENCE)
    for y in ROWS:
        _arrow(ax, (0.362, y + 0.022), (0.392, y + 0.022), S.REFERENCE, lw=0.8, rad=-0.9, scale=5.0, z=7)
    ax.text(0.375, 0.862, 'own rate target', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.REFERENCE)
    ax.plot([0.79, 0.79], [0.5, RETURN_Y], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.VIOLET, zorder=2)
    ax.plot([0.79, 0.375], [RETURN_Y, RETURN_Y], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.VIOLET, zorder=2)
    _arrow(ax, (0.375, RETURN_Y), (0.375, 0.472), S.VIOLET, lw=1.0, scale=6.0)
    ax.plot([0.762, 0.79], [0.5, 0.5], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.VIOLET, zorder=2)
    ax.text(0.582, RETURN_Y + 0.028, 'fixed random return, never the forward weights', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.VIOLET)
    ax.text(0.437, 0.165, 'the device supplies the fading trace at every crosspoint; the rest is wiring', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE, color=S.DEVICE)
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/multilayer\_results.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/multilayer\_results.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Multilayer learning outcomes."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from mrl_trace.archives import load
from . import figstyle as S

CONDITIONS = (
    ("dfa_dist", "per-neuron credit\nonly", S.COMPARATOR),
    ("dfa_homeo_dist", "with local\nhomeostasis", S.DEVICE),
    ("no_trace_homeo_dist", "zero eligibility\n(control)", S.CONTROL),
)


def _mean_sem(values):
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    return float(values.mean()), float(values.std(ddof=1) / np.sqrt(values.size))


def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    S.apply_style()
    with load("multilayer") as bundle:
        condition = np.asarray(bundle["condition"], str)
        final = np.asarray(bundle["final_reward"], float)
        run = np.asarray(bundle["run"], int)

    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.6, 2.7))
    ax = axes[0]
    for index, (key, label, colour) in enumerate(CONDITIONS):
        mean, standard_error = _mean_sem(final[condition == key])
        ax.bar(index, mean, width=0.5, yerr=standard_error, capsize=3.0, color=colour)
    ax.axhline(0.5, color=S.REFERENCE, lw=1.0, ls="--", zorder=1, label="chance")
    ax.set_xticks(range(len(CONDITIONS)))
    ax.set_xticklabels([entry[1] for entry in CONDITIONS])
    ax.set_ylabel("final reward rate")
    ax.set_ylim(0.0, 1.0)
    ax.legend(loc="upper left", frameon=False)
    S.house_grid(ax)
    S.panel(ax, "a")

    by_condition = {
        key: {int(r): float(value) for r, value in zip(run[condition == key], final[condition == key])}
        for key, _, _ in CONDITIONS
    }
    common = sorted(set.intersection(*(set(values) for values in by_condition.values())))
    contrasts = (
        ("homeostasis\nhelps",
         [by_condition["dfa_homeo_dist"][r] - by_condition["dfa_dist"][r] for r in common],
         S.DEVICE),
        ("the trace is\nnecessary",
         [by_condition["dfa_homeo_dist"][r] - by_condition["no_trace_homeo_dist"][r]
          for r in common],
         S.LINEAR),
    )
    ax = axes[1]
    for index, (label, differences, colour) in enumerate(contrasts):
        mean, standard_error = _mean_sem(differences)
        ax.bar(index, mean, width=0.5, yerr=standard_error, capsize=3.0, color=colour)
    ax.set_xticks([0, 1])
    ax.set_xticklabels([entry[0] for entry in contrasts])
    ax.set_ylabel("paired difference in final reward")
    ax.set_xlim(-0.6, 1.6)
    S.house_grid(ax)
    S.panel(ax, "b")
    fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/reliability.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/reliability.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np
from .data import device_arrays, fit_rows as deposited_fit_rows
from .data import reference_traces, role_swap_effects
from .figstyle import PALETTE, apply_style
from .learned import box
CONV = (('full_duration_floor', 'full-duration final 10%', PALETTE['device']), ('free_floor', 'fitted over selected interval', PALETTE['exponential']), ('window_tail_floor', 'selected-interval final 10%', PALETTE['linear']))
GEO, ACCT = ('h2', 'autocorr_adjusted')
FIGSIZE = (7.0, 5.1)
LABEL, TICK, NOTE = (7.6, 7.4, 7.4)
INK, GRID, GREY, REFC = (PALETTE['ink'], PALETTE['grid'], PALETTE['control'], PALETTE['reference'])

def fit_rows():
    return deposited_fit_rows()

def _se(v):
    n = len(v)
    m = sum(v) / n
    sd = math.sqrt(sum(((x - m) ** 2 for x in v)) / (n - 1))
    return (m, sd / math.sqrt(n))

def kww(t, tau, beta):
    return np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(np.asarray(t, float), 1e-12) / tau, beta))

def panel_a(ax, rows):
    for conv, lab, col in CONV:
        wins = sorted({float(r['window_s']) for r in rows})
        m = [np.mean([float(r['beta']) for r in rows if r['convention'] == conv and float(r['window_s']) == w]) for w in wins]
        sd = [np.std([float(r['beta']) for r in rows if r['convention'] == conv and float(r['window_s']) == w], ddof=1) for w in wins]
        ax.errorbar(wins, m, yerr=sd, fmt='o-', ms=3.6, lw=1.4, color=col, capsize=2.4, label=lab, zorder=3)
    ax.axhline(1.0, color=REFC, lw=1.0, ls='--', zorder=2)
    ax.text(20, 1.02, '$\\beta=1$', ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=NOTE - 1.2, color=REFC)
    ax.set_xscale('log')
    ax.set_xticks([20, 30, 60, 120, 160, 226])
    ax.set_xticklabels(['20', '30', '60', '120', '160', '226'], fontsize=TICK - 0.6)
    ax.xaxis.set_minor_locator(matplotlib.ticker.NullLocator())
    ax.set_xlabel('observation window (s)', fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.set_ylabel('fitted exponent, $\\beta$', fontsize=LABEL)
    keys = [plt.Line2D([], [], color=c, lw=1.6) for _, _, c in CONV]
    ax.legend(keys, [lab for _, lab, _ in CONV], frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, loc='upper right', handlelength=1.5, labelspacing=0.62, title='background current, $I_0$', title_fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, bbox_to_anchor=(1.0, 1.03))
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 0.6)

def panel_b(ax, rows):
    traces, fits = reference_traces()
    floor = float(np.mean(fits['floor']))
    amp = float(np.mean(fits['A']))
    for k, (t, amps, keep) in enumerate(traces):
        ax.plot(t[keep], (amps[keep] - fits['floor'][k]) / fits['A'][k], lw=0.6, color=GREY, alpha=0.8, zorder=2, label='measured repeats' if k == 0 else None)
    sel = lambda w, c, key: float(np.mean([float(r[key]) for r in rows if float(r['window_s']) == float(w) and r['convention'] == c]))
    dense = np.linspace(0.0, 228.0, 700)
    ax.plot(dense, kww(dense, sel('226.0', 'full_duration_floor', 'tau_s'), sel('226.0', 'full_duration_floor', 'beta')), lw=1.9, color=PALETTE['device'], zorder=4, label='226 s fit')
    tau30, beta30 = (sel('30.0', 'window_tail_floor', 'tau_s'), sel('30.0', 'window_tail_floor', 'beta'))
    assert abs(beta30 - 1.0743) < 0.0005, beta30
    q30 = (sel('30.0', 'window_tail_floor', 'floor_a') + sel('30.0', 'window_tail_floor', 'amplitude_a') * kww(dense, tau30, beta30) - floor) / amp
    inside = dense <= 30.0
    ax.plot(dense[inside], q30[inside], lw=1.8, color=PALETTE['linear'], zorder=5, label='30 s fit')
    ax.plot(dense[~inside], q30[~inside], lw=1.6, ls='--', color=PALETTE['linear'], zorder=5, label='extrapolated')
    ax.axvspan(0, 30, color=GRID, alpha=0.5, lw=0, zorder=0)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 228)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.05, 1.1)
    ax.set_xlabel('time after bias removal (s)', fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.set_ylabel('normalised decay, $q$', fontsize=LABEL)
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, loc='upper right', handlelength=1.6, labelspacing=0.18)
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 0.6)

def panel_c(ax):
    data = device_arrays()
    rows = []
    for run in sorted(set(data['uncertainty_run'])):
        common = (data['uncertainty_run'] == run) & (data['uncertainty_accounting'] == ACCT) & (data['uncertainty_geometry'] == GEO)
        full = common & (data['uncertainty_window_s'] == 226.0)
        short = common & (data['uncertainty_window_s'] == 30.0)
        a = (float(data['uncertainty_threshold_low'][full][0]), float(data['uncertainty_threshold_high'][full][0]))
        b = (float(data['uncertainty_threshold_low'][short][0]), float(data['uncertainty_threshold_high'][short][0]))
        rows.append((a, b, a[1] < b[0] or b[1] < a[0]))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: 0.5 * (r[0][0] + r[0][1]))
    disjoint = sum((1 for r in rows if r[2]))
    assert disjoint == 8 and len(rows) == 9, (disjoint, len(rows))
    for i, (a, b, dj) in enumerate(rows):
        if dj:
            lo, hi = (a[1], b[0]) if a[1] < b[0] else (b[1], a[0])
            ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((lo, i - 0.3), hi - lo, 0.6, facecolor=GREY, alpha=0.25, lw=0, zorder=1))
        for iv, col, lab in ((a, PALETTE['device'], '226 s'), (b, PALETTE['linear'], '30 s')):
            ax.plot(iv, [i, i], lw=2.4, color=col, solid_capstyle='butt', zorder=3, label=lab if i == 0 else None)
            for x in iv:
                ax.plot([x, x], [i - 0.17, i + 0.17], lw=1.0, color=col, zorder=4)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.7, len(rows) - 0.3)
    ax.set_yticks([])
    ax.set_ylabel('measurements, ranked', fontsize=LABEL)
    ax.set_xlabel('credit threshold $r^{*}$ at $(3,20)$ s', fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=NOTE - 1.6, loc='lower center', bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, 1.0), ncol=2, handlelength=1.3, columnspacing=1.4, borderaxespad=0.0)
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 0.6)

def panel_d(ax):
    g = role_swap_effects()
    labs = (('h1', '$(1.5,\\,11)$ s'), ('h2', '$(3.5,\\,19)$ s'))
    for k, (pair, lab) in enumerate(labs):
        runs = sorted(set(g['short_30_window_tail', pair]) & set(g['full_226_window_tail', pair]))
        d = [g['short_30_window_tail', pair][run] - g['full_226_window_tail', pair][run] for run in runs]
        m, _ = _se(d)
        box(ax, d, len(labs) - 1 - k, PALETTE['linear'] if m > 0 else PALETTE['device'], vertical=False, width=0.4)
    ax.axvline(0.0, color=INK, lw=1.0, ls='--', zorder=4)
    ax.set_yticks(range(len(labs)))
    ax.set_yticklabels([lab for _, lab in labs][::-1], fontsize=TICK)
    ax.set_ylim(-0.55, len(labs) - 0.45)
    ax.set_xlim(-0.012, 0.198)
    ax.set_xlabel('$\\Delta g_{\\mathrm{trunc}}=g(q_{30})-g(q_{226})$', fontsize=LABEL - 0.4)
    ax.tick_params(labelsize=TICK - 0.6)

def figure():
    apply_style()
    rows = fit_rows()
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=FIGSIZE)
    panel_b(axes[0, 0], rows)
    panel_a(axes[0, 1], rows)
    panel_c(axes[1, 0])
    panel_d(axes[1, 1])
    for ax, letter in zip(axes.ravel(), 'abcd'):
        ax.text(-0.16, 1.04, letter, transform=ax.transAxes, fontsize=8.2, fontweight='bold', va='bottom', color=INK)
    fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/three\_factor.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/three\_factor.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import numpy as np
from matplotlib.patches import Circle, FancyArrowPatch, Rectangle
from . import figstyle as S
TITLE, LABEL, NOTE = (7.4, 6.9, 6.9)
OXIDE, METAL = ('#f2f4f6', '#d9dee3')

def _blank(ax, title):
    ax.set_xlim(0, 1)
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1)
    ax.set_axis_off()
    ax.text(0.0, 1.06, title, ha='left', va='bottom', fontsize=TITLE, color=S.INK)
    return ax

def _arrow(ax, a, b, colour, *, lw=0.9, style='-|>', ls='-', rad=0.0, scale=6.5, z=5):
    ax.add_patch(FancyArrowPatch(a, b, arrowstyle=style, mutation_scale=scale, lw=lw, color=colour, linestyle=ls, shrinkA=0.5, shrinkB=0.5, connectionstyle=f'arc3,rad={rad}', zorder=z))

def _cell(ax, x0, y0, w, h, *, held, total=4, escaping=0, seed=0):
    bar = 0.16 * h
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((x0, y0 + h - bar), w, bar, facecolor=METAL, edgecolor=S.INK, lw=0.8, zorder=3))
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((x0, y0), w, bar, facecolor=METAL, edgecolor=S.INK, lw=0.8, zorder=3))
    ax.add_patch(Rectangle((x0, y0 + bar), w, h - 2 * bar, facecolor=OXIDE, edgecolor=S.INK, lw=0.8, zorder=2))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    xs = x0 + w * np.linspace(0.16, 0.84, total)
    ys = y0 + bar + (h - 2 * bar) * (0.3 + 0.4 * rng.random(total))
    for index, (x, y) in enumerate(zip(xs, ys)):
        ax.add_patch(Circle((x, y), 0.014 * w / 0.3, facecolor='white', edgecolor=S.CONTROL, lw=0.7, zorder=4))
        if index < held:
            ax.add_patch(Circle((x, y), 0.008 * w / 0.3, facecolor=S.DEVICE, edgecolor='none', zorder=6))
        if held <= index < held + escaping:
            _arrow(ax, (x, y + 0.02), (x, y0 + h + 0.03), S.DEVICE, lw=0.8, scale=5.5)
    return (xs, ys)

def _spikes(ax, x, y, colour, n=2, dx=0.022, height=0.05):
    for k in range(n):
        ax.vlines(x + k * dx, y - height / 2, y + height / 2, color=colour, lw=1.1, zorder=6)

def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    S.apply_style()
    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(6.05, 2.05))
    axa = _blank(fig.add_axes([0.015, 0.06, 0.285, 0.74]), '(a)  a coincidence traps charge')
    axb = _blank(fig.add_axes([0.36, 0.06, 0.285, 0.74]), '(b)  the charge leaks out alone')
    axc = _blank(fig.add_axes([0.705, 0.06, 0.285, 0.74]), '(c)  reward commits what is left')
    _cell(axa, 0.32, 0.46, 0.34, 0.38, held=4, seed=1)
    axa.add_patch(Circle((0.07, 0.65), 0.045, facecolor='white', edgecolor=S.INK, lw=1.0, zorder=4))
    axa.plot([0.115, 0.32], [0.65, 0.65], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.INK, zorder=2)
    _spikes(axa, 0.17, 0.65, S.DEVICE)
    axa.text(0.07, 0.56, 'pre', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    axa.plot([0.66, 0.85], [0.65, 0.65], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.INK, zorder=2)
    axa.add_patch(Circle((0.9, 0.65), 0.045, facecolor='white', edgecolor=S.INK, lw=1.0, zorder=4))
    _spikes(axa, 0.7, 0.65, S.DEVICE)
    axa.text(0.9, 0.56, 'post', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    for x in (0.4, 0.5, 0.6):
        _arrow(axa, (x, 0.815), (x, 0.735), S.DEVICE, lw=0.8, scale=5.5)
    axa.text(0.49, 0.34, 'pre and post fire together,\nso charge is captured', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE, color=S.DEVICE)
    _cell(axb, 0.02, 0.5, 0.32, 0.3, held=2, escaping=2, seed=1)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 200)
    q = np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(t, 1e-09) * 2.4, 0.62))
    bx, by, bw, bh = (0.6, 0.5, 0.3, 0.3)
    _arrow(axb, (bx, by), (bx + bw + 0.04, by), S.INK, lw=0.8, style='-|>', scale=6)
    _arrow(axb, (bx, by), (bx, by + bh + 0.05), S.INK, lw=0.8, style='-|>', scale=6)
    axb.plot(bx + t * bw, by + q * bh * 0.92, '-', lw=1.5, color=S.DEVICE, zorder=4)
    for frac in (0.16, 0.7):
        height = float(np.exp(-np.power(frac * 2.4, 0.62))) * bh * 0.92
        _arrow(axb, (bx + frac * bw, by + height), (bx + frac * bw, by + 0.012), S.REFERENCE, lw=0.9, scale=6)
    axb.text(bx - 0.075, by + bh * 0.55, 'charge held', ha='center', va='center', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK, rotation=90)
    axb.text(bx + bw + 0.05, by - 0.03, 'time', ha='right', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    axb.text(bx, by - 0.03, 'coincidence', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    axb.text(0.46, 0.32, 'no input arrives, yet\nthe charge keeps leaving', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE, color=S.DEVICE)
    for x0, held, when in ((0.05, 3, 'soon after'), (0.55, 1, 'much later')):
        _cell(axc, x0, 0.5, 0.3, 0.3, held=held, seed=1)
        axc.plot([x0 - 0.02, x0 + 0.32], [0.92, 0.92], '-', lw=1.0, color=S.REFERENCE, zorder=2)
        _arrow(axc, (x0 + 0.15, 0.92), (x0 + 0.15, 0.815), S.REFERENCE, lw=0.8, ls=(0, (2.0, 1.4)), scale=5.5)
        axc.text(x0 + 0.15, 0.44, when, ha='center', va='top', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.INK)
    axc.text(0.45, 0.925, 'reward', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=LABEL, color=S.REFERENCE)
    axc.text(0.47, 0.32, 'how far the weight moves follows\nwhat the traps still hold', ha='center', va='top', fontsize=NOTE, color=S.DEVICE)
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/threshold\_witness.py}
\index{src/mrl\_trace/plotting/threshold\_witness.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
from __future__ import annotations
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
from mrl_trace.kernels import scalar_insufficiency_witness
from . import figstyle as S
ANCHOR_RETENTION_S = 23.043258124936226

def figure():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    witness = scalar_insufficiency_witness(ANCHOR_RETENTION_S)
    rows = witness['kernels']
    betas = [row['beta'] for row in rows]
    thresholds = [row['credit_threshold'] for row in rows]
    low, high = witness['disagreement_interval']
    INTERVAL = S.PALETTE['rose']
    S.apply_style()
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.9, 2.9))
    example_r = 1.6
    ax.plot(betas, thresholds, marker='o', ms=4.0, color=S.DEVICE, zorder=3)
    for beta, threshold in zip(betas, thresholds):
        near_line = abs(threshold - example_r) < 0.05
        offset, align = ((10, -5), 'left') if near_line else ((5, 3), 'left')
        ax.annotate(f'{threshold:.3f}', (beta, threshold), textcoords='offset points', xytext=offset, ha=align, fontsize=5.8, color=S.PALETTE['ink'])
    span = high - low
    x0, x1 = (min(betas), max(betas))
    pad = 0.06 * (x1 - x0)
    ax.set_xlim(x0 - pad, x1 + 7.2 * pad)
    for level in (low, high):
        ax.plot([x0 - pad, x1 + 4.4 * pad], [level, level], lw=0.8, ls=(0, (3, 2)), color=INTERVAL, zorder=2)
    xa = x1 + 3.6 * pad
    ax.annotate('', xy=(xa, high), xytext=(xa, low), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='<|-|>', color=INTERVAL, lw=0.9, shrinkA=0, shrinkB=0, mutation_scale=7.0), zorder=4)
    ax.annotate(f'{span:.3f}', (xa, 0.5 * (low + high)), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(4, 0), va='center', fontsize=6.0, color=INTERVAL)
    above = [b for b, t in zip(betas, thresholds) if t > example_r]
    below = [b for b, t in zip(betas, thresholds) if t < example_r]
    ax.plot([x0 - pad, x1 + 2.0 * pad], [example_r, example_r], lw=1.0, ls=(0, (5, 2)), color=S.PALETTE['ink'], zorder=2)
    ax.annotate(f'$r = {example_r:.2f}$', (x0 - 0.4 * pad, example_r), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(2, 3), fontsize=6.0, color=S.PALETTE['ink'])
    ax.annotate('newer', (x1 + 1.1 * pad, example_r), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(0, 5), ha='center', fontsize=5.8, color=S.PALETTE['ink'])
    ax.annotate('older', (x1 + 1.1 * pad, example_r), textcoords='offset points', xytext=(0, -10), ha='center', fontsize=5.8, color=S.PALETTE['ink'])
    ax.set_xlabel('dispersion exponent $\\beta_{leak}$')
    ax.set_ylabel('credit threshold $r^{\\ast}$')
    ax.set_title(f'all five share $\\langle t\\rangle$ = {ANCHOR_RETENTION_S:.2f} s', fontsize=7.0)
    S.house_grid(ax)
    fig.tight_layout()
    return fig
\end{Verbatim}
\section{Reproduction and data-preparation tools}
\label{sec:tools}
\subsection{tools/prepare\_data.py}
\index{tools/prepare\_data.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Prepare separately downloaded external measurements."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import curve_fit

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from mrl_trace.archives import external_cache

PAIRS = (("A", "B", 7.1), ("D", "E", 27.1), ("H", "I", 34.5),
         ("K", "L", 41.8), ("N", "O", 45.3))
TRUNCATED_FRACTION = 30.0 / 226.0


def _sheet(path: Path, name: str) -> dict[int, dict[str, str]]:
    main = "{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"
    office = "{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships}"
    package = "{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships}"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as workbook:
        rels = {
            node.get("Id"): node.get("Target")
            for node in ET.fromstring(workbook.read("xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels"))
            .iter(package + "Relationship")
        }
        target = None
        for node in ET.fromstring(workbook.read("xl/workbook.xml")).iter(main + "sheet"):
            if node.get("name") == name:
                target = "xl/" + rels[node.get(office + "id")].lstrip("/").replace("xl/", "")
                break
        if target is None:
            raise ValueError(f"worksheet {name!r} was not found")
        try:
            strings = [
                "".join(text.text or "" for text in item.iter(main + "t"))
                for item in ET.fromstring(workbook.read("xl/sharedStrings.xml")).iter(main + "si")
            ]
        except KeyError:
            strings = []
        rows: dict[int, dict[str, str]] = {}
        for cell in ET.fromstring(workbook.read(target)).iter(main + "c"):
            value = cell.find(main + "v")
            if value is None or value.text is None:
                continue
            parsed = strings[int(value.text)] if cell.get("t") == "s" and strings else value.text
            match = re.match(r"([A-Z]+)(\d+)", cell.get("r", ""))
            if match:
                rows.setdefault(int(match.group(2)), {})[match.group(1)] = parsed
    return rows


def _column(rows, letter: str) -> np.ndarray:
    values = []
    for row in sorted(rows):
        if row == 1 or letter not in rows[row]:
            continue
        try:
            values.append(float(rows[row][letter]))
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return np.asarray(values, dtype=float)


def _model(t, floor, amplitude, tau, beta):
    return floor + amplitude * np.exp(-np.power(np.maximum(t, 0.0) / tau, beta))


def _fit(t, current) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
    span = max(float(t[-1] - t[0]), 1e-6)
    start, end = float(current[0]), float(current[-1])
    params, _ = curve_fit(
        _model, t, current,
        p0=(end, start - end, span / 3.0, 0.6),
        bounds=((-np.inf, -np.inf, 1e-9, 0.05),
                (np.inf, np.inf, 1e6, 3.0)),
        maxfev=200_000,
    )
    return tuple(map(float, params))


def prepare_external(workbook: Path) -> Path:
    metadata = json.loads((ROOT / "data" / "external.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    rows = _sheet(workbook, metadata["worksheet"])
    flat_record, flat_time, flat_current, flat_porosity = [], [], [], []
    trace_record, trace_porosity, beta_complete, beta_truncated = [], [], [], []
    floor_complete, amp_complete, tau_complete = [], [], []
    floor_truncated, amp_truncated, tau_truncated = [], [], []

    for time_column, current_column, porosity in PAIRS:
        time = _column(rows, time_column)
        current = _column(rows, current_column)
        count = min(len(time), len(current))
        time, current = time[:count], current[:count]
        time = time - time[0]
        current = current / current[0]
        name = f"porosity_{porosity:.1f}"
        cut = TRUNCATED_FRACTION * time[-1]
        short = time <= cut
        complete = _fit(time, current)
        truncated = _fit(time[short], current[short])

        flat_record.extend([name] * count)
        flat_time.extend(time)
        flat_current.extend(current)
        flat_porosity.extend([porosity] * count)
        trace_record.append(name)
        trace_porosity.append(porosity)
        floor_complete.append(complete[0])
        amp_complete.append(complete[1])
        tau_complete.append(complete[2])
        beta_complete.append(complete[3])
        floor_truncated.append(truncated[0])
        amp_truncated.append(truncated[1])
        tau_truncated.append(truncated[2])
        beta_truncated.append(truncated[3])

    target = external_cache()
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    np.savez_compressed(
        target,
        record=np.asarray(flat_record),
        time_s=np.asarray(flat_time),
        current_normalised=np.asarray(flat_current),
        porosity_percent=np.asarray(flat_porosity),
        trace_record=np.asarray(trace_record),
        trace_porosity_percent=np.asarray(trace_porosity),
        floor_complete=np.asarray(floor_complete),
        amplitude_complete=np.asarray(amp_complete),
        tau_complete_s=np.asarray(tau_complete),
        beta_complete=np.asarray(beta_complete),
        floor_truncated=np.asarray(floor_truncated),
        amplitude_truncated=np.asarray(amp_truncated),
        tau_truncated_s=np.asarray(tau_truncated),
        beta_truncated=np.asarray(beta_truncated),
        truncated_fraction=np.asarray(TRUNCATED_FRACTION),
    )
    print(f"prepared five external decays: {target}")
    for porosity, complete, truncated in zip(
            trace_porosity, beta_complete, beta_truncated):
        print(f"  {porosity:4.1f}%: beta {complete:.4f} -> {truncated:.4f}")
    return target


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("dataset", choices=("external",))
    parser.add_argument("workbook", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not args.workbook.is_file():
        raise SystemExit(f"workbook not found: {args.workbook}")
    prepare_external(args.workbook.resolve())
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{tools/quantities.py}
\index{tools/quantities.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
"""Calculate the principal reported quantities from the deposited rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import sys
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from mrl_trace.archives import load
from mrl_trace.kernels import scalar_insufficiency_witness
from mrl_trace.statistics import exact_sign_flip_p, holm, mean_and_standard_error


def _role_effects():
    with load("credit") as z:
        cells = defaultdict(dict)
        for run, arm, timing, role, value in zip(
                z["run"], z["description"], z["geometry"], z["role"], z["aulc"]):
            cells[(str(arm), str(timing), int(run))][str(role)] = float(value)
    effects = {}
    for (arm, timing, run), roles in cells.items():
        if len(roles) == 2:
            effects[(arm, timing, run)] = (
                roles["recent_informative"] - roles["old_informative"])
    return effects


def _contrast(effects, upper, lower, timing):
    runs = sorted({r for arm, t, r in effects if t == timing and arm in (upper, lower)})
    return [effects[(upper, timing, r)] - effects[(lower, timing, r)] for r in runs]


def values() -> dict:
    with load("device") as z:
        accepted_measurements = int(np.count_nonzero(z["accepted"]))
        screened_measurements = int(z["accepted"].size - accepted_measurements)
        fit_cells = int(z["fit_beta"].size)
        mask = (z["fit_window_s"] == 226.0) & (z["fit_convention"] == "full_duration_floor")
        tau = float(z["fit_tau_s"][mask].mean())
        beta = float(z["fit_beta"][mask].mean())
        retention = (tau / beta) * math.gamma(1.0 / beta)
        beta_span = (float(z["fit_beta"].min()), float(z["fit_beta"].max()))
        means = []
        for convention in sorted(set(map(str, z["fit_convention"]))):
            for window in sorted(set(z["fit_window_s"].tolist())):
                cell = (z["fit_convention"] == convention) & (z["fit_window_s"] == window)
                means.append(float(z["fit_beta"][cell].mean()))
        condition_span = (min(means), max(means))

    witness = scalar_insufficiency_witness(retention)
    effects = _role_effects()
    kernel = {t: _contrast(effects, "full_226_full_duration", "frozen_exponential", t)
              for t in ("h1", "h2")}
    trunc = {t: _contrast(effects, "short_30_window_tail", "full_226_window_tail", t)
             for t in ("h1", "h2")}
    timing = [a - b for a, b in zip(kernel["h1"], kernel["h2"])]
    trunc_cross = [a - b for a, b in zip(trunc["h1"], trunc["h2"])]
    trunc_raw = [exact_sign_flip_p(trunc["h1"]), exact_sign_flip_p(trunc["h2"]),
                 exact_sign_flip_p(trunc_cross)]
    trunc_holm = holm(trunc_raw)

    with load("multilayer") as z:
        per = defaultdict(dict)
        for run, condition, reward in zip(z["run"], z["condition"], z["final_reward"]):
            per[int(run)][str(condition)] = float(reward)
        runs = sorted(per)
        homeostasis = [per[r]["dfa_homeo_dist"] - per[r]["dfa_dist"] for r in runs]
        necessity = [per[r]["dfa_homeo_dist"] - per[r]["no_trace_homeo_dist"] for r in runs]

    out = {
        "accepted_measurements": accepted_measurements,
        "screened_measurements": screened_measurements,
        "fit_cells": fit_cells,
        "tau_s": tau,
        "beta": beta,
        "mean_retention_s": retention,
        "beta_span": beta_span,
        "condition_mean_span": condition_span,
        "equal_retention_threshold_interval": tuple(witness["disagreement_interval"]),
        "kernel_h1": (*mean_and_standard_error(kernel["h1"]),),
        "kernel_h2": (*mean_and_standard_error(kernel["h2"]),),
        "kernel_cross_timing": (*mean_and_standard_error(timing), exact_sign_flip_p(timing)),
        "trunc_h1": (*mean_and_standard_error(trunc["h1"]), trunc_holm[0]),
        "trunc_h2": (*mean_and_standard_error(trunc["h2"]), trunc_holm[1]),
        "trunc_cross_timing": (*mean_and_standard_error(trunc_cross), trunc_holm[2]),
        "homeostasis": (*mean_and_standard_error(homeostasis), exact_sign_flip_p(homeostasis)),
        "eligibility_required": (*mean_and_standard_error(necessity), exact_sign_flip_p(necessity)),
    }
    return out


def print_summary() -> None:
    q = values()
    print(f"  measurements: {q['accepted_measurements']} accepted, "
          f"{q['screened_measurements']} screened")
    print(f"  full-duration kernel: tau={q['tau_s']:.4f} s, beta={q['beta']:.4f}, "
          f"mean retention={q['mean_retention_s']:.4f} s")
    print(f"  beta over fits: {q['beta_span'][0]:.4f} to {q['beta_span'][1]:.4f}")
    print(f"  equal-retention thresholds: {q['equal_retention_threshold_interval'][0]:.4f} "
          f"to {q['equal_retention_threshold_interval'][1]:.4f}")
    for label in ("kernel_h1", "kernel_h2", "kernel_cross_timing",
                  "trunc_h1", "trunc_h2", "trunc_cross_timing",
                  "homeostasis", "eligibility_required"):
        value = q[label]
        suffix = f", p={value[2]:.5g}" if len(value) == 3 else ""
        print(f"  {label.replace('_', ' ')}: {value[0]:+.4f} +/- {value[1]:.4f}{suffix}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print_summary()
\end{Verbatim}
\subsection{tools/reproduce.py}
\index{tools/reproduce.py}
\begin{Verbatim}
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reproduce figures or rerun the learning pipelines."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path[:0] = [str(ROOT), str(ROOT / "src"), str(ROOT / "tools")]

from mrl_trace import identification, multilayer, role_swap
from mrl_trace.figures import FIGURES, save
import quantities

PROFILES = ("archived", "reduced", "published")
BUDGETS = {
    "reduced": {
        "identification": {"runs": ("545", "546", "547"), "windows": (30.0, 226.0)},
        "role_swap": {"lanes": 4, "trials": 100, "stream": "reduced"},
        "multilayer": {"lanes": 4, "trials": 200},
    },
    "published": {
        "identification": {"runs": None, "windows": identification.WINDOWS_S},
        "role_swap": {"lanes": 16, "trials": 500, "stream": "published"},
        "multilayer": {"lanes": 20, "trials": 2000},
    },
}


def render_figures() -> tuple[int, int]:
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from mrl_trace.plotting import figures as producers

    rendered = skipped = 0
    for stem, function_name in FIGURES.items():
        try:
            figure = getattr(producers, function_name)()
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            print(f"  skip {stem}: {exc}")
            skipped += 1
            continue
        save(figure, stem)
        plt.close(figure)
        print(f"  figure: {stem}")
        rendered += 1
    return rendered, skipped


def _rows_to_arrays(rows: list[dict]) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    if not rows:
        return {}
    return {key: np.asarray([row[key] for row in rows]) for key in rows[0]}


def _write_result(profile: str, stage: str, result: dict, elapsed: float) -> None:
    target = ROOT / "outputs" / profile
    target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    arrays = _rows_to_arrays(result["rows"])
    if stage == "multilayer":
        for name, curve in result["curves"].items():
            arrays[f"curve_{name}"] = np.asarray(curve)
    np.savez_compressed(target / f"{stage}.npz", **arrays)
    summary = {
        "profile": profile,
        "stage": stage,
        "seconds": elapsed,
        "rows": len(result["rows"]),
        "protocol": result["protocol"],
    }
    if "endpoints" in result:
        summary["endpoints"] = result["endpoints"]
    (target / f"{stage}.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(summary, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _print_role_swap(result: dict) -> None:
    for family, block in result["endpoints"].items():
        for timing, cell in block["per_geometry"].items():
            print(f"    {family:9s} {timing}: {cell['mean']:+.4f}, "
                  f"p={cell['sign_flip_p_two_sided']:.5g}")


def _print_multilayer(result: dict) -> None:
    finals = result["finals"]
    homeostasis = np.mean(finals["dfa_homeo_dist"] - finals["dfa_dist"])
    eligibility = np.mean(finals["dfa_homeo_dist"] - finals["no_trace_homeo_dist"])
    print(f"    homeostasis: {homeostasis:+.4f}")
    print(f"    eligibility required: {eligibility:+.4f}")


def _run_identification(profile: str) -> None:
    budget = BUDGETS[profile]["identification"]
    print(f"\nidentification: {len(budget['runs']) if budget['runs'] else 9} measurements")
    started = time.perf_counter()
    ordinary = identification.fit_relaxations(
        progress=lambda label: print(f"  run {label}"),
        robust=False,
        **budget,
    )
    robust = identification.fit_relaxations(robust=True, **budget)
    ordinary["rows"].extend(robust["rows"])
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
    _write_result(profile, "identification", ordinary, elapsed)
    full = [row for row in ordinary["rows"]
            if not row["robust"] and row["window_s"] == 226.0
            and row["convention"] == "full_duration_floor"]
    if full:
        print(f"    tau={np.mean([row['tau_s'] for row in full]):.4f} s, "
              f"beta={np.mean([row['beta'] for row in full]):.4f}")
    print(f"  completed in {elapsed:.1f} s")


def run_training(profile: str) -> int:
    _run_identification(profile)
    jobs = (
        ("role_swap", role_swap.run_role_swap, BUDGETS[profile]["role_swap"],
         _print_role_swap),
        ("multilayer", multilayer.run_multilayer, BUDGETS[profile]["multilayer"],
         _print_multilayer),
    )
    for name, function, budget, report in jobs:
        print(f"\n{name}: {budget['lanes']} runs x {budget['trials']} trials")
        started = time.perf_counter()
        result = function(progress=lambda label: print(f"  {label}"), **budget)
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
        report(result)
        _write_result(profile, name, result, elapsed)
        print(f"  completed in {elapsed:.1f} s")
    return 0


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("profile", nargs="?", default="archived", choices=PROFILES)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    print(f"profile: {args.profile}")
    if args.profile == "archived":
        quantities.print_summary()
        rendered, skipped = render_figures()
        print(f"\n{rendered} figures regenerated; {skipped} external figure names skipped.")
        return 0
    return run_training(args.profile)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
\end{Verbatim}

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from mrl_trace.archives import load


def test_device_archive():
    with load("device") as data:
        assert data["run"].shape == (10,)
        assert data["accepted"].sum() == 9
        assert data["run"][~data["accepted"]].tolist() == ["554"]
        assert data["fit_beta"].shape == (162,)
        assert data["robust_beta"].shape == (162,)
        assert data["uncertainty_beta_low"].shape == (72,)


def test_learning_archives_use_run():
    for name, rows in (("credit", 320), ("multilayer", 60)):
        with load(name) as data:
            assert "run" in data.files
            assert "seed" not in data.files
            assert data["run"].shape == (rows,)


def test_archives_are_pickle_free():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "data"
    for path in root.glob("*.npz"):
        with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as data:
            for key in data.files:
                assert data[key].dtype != object
\end{Verbatim}

from pathlib import Path

import nbformat

from mrl_trace.figures import FIGURES
from mrl_trace.plotting import figures


def test_all_first_party_figure_producers_draw():
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

    external = {"window_external", "fig_s1_window_external"}
    for stem, function_name in FIGURES.items():
        if stem in external:
            continue
        figure = getattr(figures, function_name)()
        figure.canvas.draw()
        plt.close(figure)


def test_notebooks_are_executed():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "notebooks"
    paths = sorted(root.glob("*.ipynb"))
    assert len(paths) == 3
    for path in paths:
        notebook = nbformat.read(path, as_version=4)
        code = [cell for cell in notebook.cells if cell.cell_type == "code"]
        assert code
        assert all(cell.execution_count is not None for cell in code)
        assert any(cell.outputs for cell in code)
\end{Verbatim}

from mrl_trace import identification, multilayer, role_swap


def test_reduced_identification_uses_the_complete_driver():
    result = identification.fit_relaxations(
        runs=("545",), windows=(30.0, 226.0), robust=False)
    assert len(result["rows"]) == 6
    assert {row["convention"] for row in result["rows"]} == set(identification.CONVENTIONS)


def test_learning_drivers_accept_smoke_test_budgets():
    role = role_swap.run_role_swap(lanes=2, trials=2, stream="reduced")
    assert len(role["rows"]) == 40
    assert all("run" in row and "seed" not in row for row in role["rows"])

    deep = multilayer.run_multilayer(lanes=2, trials=2)
    assert len(deep["rows"]) == 6
    assert all("run" in row and "seed" not in row for row in deep["rows"])
